        }

        private FCResultType flowChart5_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.PalletCode1 = "A";
            SysPara.PalletCode2 = "B";
            SysPara.PalletCode3 = "C";
            SysPara.PalletCode4 = "D";
            SysPara.PalletCode5 = "E";
            SysPara.PalletCode6 = "F";
            if (IsDryCycleMode)
            {
                SysPara.R1ScanResult = true;
                //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode2);
                //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode3);

                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode2);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode3);

                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode4);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode5);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode6);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            string[] codes = SysPara.QRCode1.Split(new char[] { ',' });
            if (codes.Count() < 3)
            {
                SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;
            }
            else
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < codes.Length; i++)
                {

                    for (int j = i + 1; j < codes.Length; j++)
                    {

                        if (codes[i] == codes[j])
                        {
                            SysPara.R1ScanResult = false;
                        }

                    }
                }
            }




            if (SysPara.R1ScanResult)
            {

                if (QRScanNum == 2)
                {
                    SysPara.PalletCode1 = codes[2];
                    SysPara.PalletCode2 = codes[1];
                    SysPara.PalletCode3 = codes[0];
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.PalletCode1 = codes[0];
                    SysPara.PalletCode2 = codes[1];
                    SysPara.PalletCode3 = codes[2];

                }

                string temStr = "";
                if (codes[0].Contains("01"))
                {
                    temStr = codes[0].Substring(0, 8);
                    SysPara.PalletCode4 = temStr + "07";
                    SysPara.PalletCode5 = temStr + "08";
                    SysPara.PalletCode6 = temStr + "09";
                }
                else
                {
                    temStr = codes[0].Substring(0, 8);
                    SysPara.PalletCode4 = temStr + "10";
                    SysPara.PalletCode5 = temStr + "11";
                    SysPara.PalletCode6 = temStr + "12";
                }
                //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode2);
                //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode3);
                //bool bcord = SysPara.TempPalletCode.Any(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode2);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode3);

                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode4);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode5);
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode6);


                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301");
                return FCResultType.CASE1;

                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Confirm whether to scan the code again,yes or no?", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                else
                {

                    string str = string.Format("The scan result is：{0}", SysPara.QRCode1);
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);
                    if (QRScanNum == 2)
                    {
                        SysPara.PalletCode1 = codes[2];
                        SysPara.PalletCode2 = codes[1];
                        SysPara.PalletCode3 = codes[0];
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.PalletCode1 = codes[0];
                        SysPara.PalletCode2 = codes[1];
                        SysPara.PalletCode3 = codes[2];
                    }
                    //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                    //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode2);
                    //SysPara.Que_pallet_Code.Enqueue(SysPara.PalletCode3);
                    SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode1);
                    SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode2);
                    SysPara.TempPalletCode.Add(SysPara.PalletCode3);

                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }

            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart6_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //scanPosName <= 2
            if (SysPara.TempPalletCode.Count < 6)
            {
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            else
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < SysPara.TempPalletCode.Count; i++)
                {
                    string str = string.Format((i + 1).ToString() + "#Product QRCode result is：{0}", SysPara.TempPalletCode[i]);
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str, SysPara.bEnableGeneralSaveLog);

                }
                cacheTightenPos.Clear();
                OB_CamLight.On();
                if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 0;
                else
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 6;
                if (IsOnLED)
                {
                    MiddleLayer.SystemF.OB_FluorescentLight.On();
                }
                ProductImages.Clear();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
        }



        private FCResultType flowChart9_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off(200) &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On(200) &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off(200) &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On(200))
            {
                if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_DownPreProtection.On())
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1701");

                }
                else
                {
                    if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.OB_FluorescentLight.GetState())
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.SystemF.OB_FluorescentLight.Off();
                        IsOnLED = true;
                    }
                    else { IsOnLED = false; }
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(5000))
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1017");
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
        double posX = 0, posY = 0, posZ = 0;

        //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v2.1.0 opciones de velocidad (EXPERIMENTAL). Ver Classes\VelocidadV2.cs.
        //  Ninguna cambia un movimiento, un cilindro, el atornillado, el pallet ni el MES.
        //
        //  Opcion 2: el contador de tornillos (CurrentCount, alarma 1307 "tornillos por acabarse") se
        //  sigue actualizando en memoria en cada tornillo, que es de donde lo lee la revision. Solo el
        //  guardado de la receta a disco se hace al terminar el pallet (flowChart30) y al inicializar.
        //  Si se apaga la PC a media pallet, se pierden como maximo esas cuentas del contador.
        private bool contadorPorGuardar = false;

        private void GuardarContador()
        {
            if (global::Alpha.Classes.VelocidadV2.GuardarFueraDelFlujo)
            {
                contadorPorGuardar = true;
                return;
            }
            WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
        }

        private void GuardarContadorPendiente()
        {
            if (!contadorPorGuardar) return;
            contadorPorGuardar = false;
            try
            {
                WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                global::Alpha.Classes.VelocidadV2.Log("Velocidad v2.1.0: no se pudo guardar el contador de tornillos (" + ex.Message + ")");
            }
        }

        //  Opcion 2: la BMP de la foto se escribe en otro hilo, con una copia de la imagen (TakePicture
        //  entrega una imagen nueva en cada foto). Mismo nombre y carpeta que el proveedor. Regresa false
        //  si la opcion esta apagada o no se pudo copiar: entonces se guarda como siempre.
        private bool GuardarBmpEnSegundoPlano(CogImage8Grey imagen)
        {
            if (!global::Alpha.Classes.VelocidadV2.GuardarFueraDelFlujo || imagen == null) return false;
            try
            {
                ICogImage copia = imagen.CopyBase(CogImageCopyModeConstants.CopyPixels);
                string carpeta = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\BMP\\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\";
                string nombre = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.screwIndex + ".bmp";
                Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
                {
                    try
                    {
                        if (!Directory.Exists(carpeta)) Directory.CreateDirectory(carpeta);
                        using (CogImageFileBMP archivo = new CogImageFileBMP())
                        {
                            archivo.Open(carpeta + nombre, CogImageFileModeConstants.Write);
                            archivo.Append(copia);
                            archivo.Close();
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas, $"Save BMP Error:{ex}", true);
                    }
                });
                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        //  Opcion 3 (foto en paralelo). v2.1.0.1: con la vision de la pieza YA TERMINADA, el eje arranca hacia la
        //  posicion de la SIGUIENTE pieza (antes arrancaba durante la vision y las piezas 1-5 salian NG): mismo movimiento que
        //  flowChart11 (misma tabla GrabPosition, mismo Z, sin subir a seguridad), solo que antes.
        //  flowChart11 corre despues como siempre: si el eje sigue en camino, StartMoveFlow no hace nada
        //  (ya hay un movimiento en marcha) y flowChart12 espera a que llegue; si ya llego, se repite el
        //  movimiento al mismo punto y no se mueve. Resultado, alarmas y datos de la foto: sin cambios.
        //  GUARDAS: prensa del pallet abajo (igual que flowChart11), cilindro del atornillador ARRIBA,
        //  cilindro de succion ARRIBA y eje quieto. Nunca despues de la ultima foto del pallet: de ahi
        //  sigue el atornillado, que no se adelanta. Ante cualquier error se apaga hasta inicializar.
        private bool fotoParalelaApagada = false;

        private void AdelantarSiguienteFoto()
        {
            if (!global::Alpha.Classes.VelocidadV2.FotoEnParalelo || fotoParalelaApagada) return;
            try
            {
                if (cacheTightenPos.Count + 3 >= 18) return;   // ultima foto del pallet
                bool guardas = MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                               MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                               MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                               MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                               IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                               IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off() &&
                               IB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
                if (!guardas) return;
                if (motorMoveCtrl.BautoRun || !motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete) return;
                string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, "PosName" + (SysPara.screwIndex + 1));
                if (pos == null || pos.Length < 5) return;
                double x = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                double y = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                double z = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = false;
                motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = z;
                motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(x, y, z);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                fotoParalelaApagada = true;
                global::Alpha.Classes.VelocidadV2.Log("Velocidad v2.1.0: foto en paralelo apagada hasta inicializar (" + ex.Message + ")");
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart13_FlowRun(object sender,EventArgs e)
        {
            SaveImage image = new SaveImage();
            SysPara.visionPos = new VisionPos();
            try
            {
                double exposure2 =GetSettingValue("MSet","DisChargeExpTime");
                // CCD Take Picture
                if (!H1_DischargePos.TakePicture(exposure2))
                {
                    SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    image.Isok = false;
                    ProductImages.Add(image);
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1204");
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v2.1.0.1: AdelantarSiguienteFoto ya NO va aqui. Con el eje
                //  moviendose mientras corria la vision, las piezas 1-5 salieron sin barrenos (no se
                //  atornillaron, iban a NG); solo la 6 (sin adelanto) salio bien. Ahora va despues de la vision.
                H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["InputImage"].Value = H1_DischargePos.InputImage;
                H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["OrderIndex"].Value = SysPara.OrderIndex;
                bool isSave = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet","ImgSave");
                // Vision Disable
                if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet","DisableVision"))
                {
                    SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    image.Isok = false;
                    ProductImages.Add(image);
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                // BMP Save
                //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v2.1.0 opcion 2: con guardar_fuera_del_flujo la BMP se escribe en
                //  segundo plano (copia de la imagen, mismo nombre y carpeta); si no, como el proveedor.
                if (isSave && GuardarBmpEnSegundoPlano(H1_DischargePos.InputImage))
                {
                }
                else if (isSave)
                {
                    try
                    {
                        string saveImgPath =MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue( "PSet","SaveImagePath")+ "\\BMP\\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\";

                        if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath))
                        {
                            Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath);
                        }
                        string imgSaveName = DateTime.Now.ToString( "HH_mm_ss_fff") + "_" + SysPara.screwIndex + ".bmp";
                        using (CogImageFileBMP objImg = new CogImageFileBMP())
                        {
                            objImg.Open(saveImgPath + imgSaveName, CogImageFileModeConstants.Write);
                            objImg.Append( H1_DischargePos.InputImage);
                            objImg.Close();
                        }
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas,$"Save BMP Error:{ex}",true);
                    }
                }

                // Run Vision
                H1_DischargePos.RunTB();
                // UI Update
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(cogRecDisp,() =>
                {
                        if (cogRecDisp == null ||cogRecDisp.IsDisposed)
                        {
                            return;
                        }
                        cogRecDisp.Image = H1_DischargePos.GetOutput( "OutputImage") as CogImage8Grey;
                    });

                bool runStatus =(bool)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("RunStatus");
                if (H1_DischargePos.IsAccept && runStatus)
                {
                    SysPara.visionPos.x1 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X1");
                    SysPara.visionPos.x2 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X2");
                    SysPara.visionPos.x3 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X3");
                    SysPara.visionPos.y1 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y1");
                    SysPara.visionPos.y2 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y2");
                    SysPara.visionPos.y3 =(double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y3");
                    SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v1.0.6.5.3 lugar vacio del pallet: no se manda a NG
                    //  La receta de vision trae su propia revision de "hay pieza" (CheckExistProduct).
                    //  Si fue ESA la que fallo en esta foto, el lugar esta vacio: igual que antes no
                    //  se atornilla ni se reporta al MES, pero ya no se manda el gripper a sacar a la
                    //  charola NG algo que no existe. Si fallo cualquier otra cosa, NG como siempre.
                    //  v1.0.6.5.4: solo cuenta como vacio si ese bloque dio su veredicto (sin
                    //  excepcion); si no pudo medir, la pieza va a NG como antes.
                    ICogTool fallo = HerramientaQueFallo(H1_DischargePos.TB, 0);
                    string herramientaQueFallo = fallo == null ? "" : (fallo.Name ?? "");
                    bool midioSinError = fallo != null && fallo.RunStatus != null && fallo.RunStatus.Exception == null;
                    if (herramientaQueFallo == BLOQUE_HAY_PIEZA && midioSinError)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Pieza " + SysPara.screwIndex
                            + ": lugar vacio (CheckExistProduct), no se manda a NG", true);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.screwNGIndex.Enqueue(SysPara.screwIndex);
                        string porQue = herramientaQueFallo == "" ? "no encontro los 3 barrenos"
                            : herramientaQueFallo == BLOQUE_HAY_PIEZA ? "CheckExistProduct no pudo medir: " + fallo.RunStatus.Exception.Message
                            : "fallo " + herramientaQueFallo;
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Pieza " + SysPara.screwIndex
                            + ": vision NG (" + porQue + "), se manda a NG", true);
                    }
                }
                //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v2.1.0.1 opcion 3: hasta aqui, con la vision YA terminada y
                //  sus resultados leidos, el eje puede arrancar a la siguiente pieza (con guardas).
                AdelantarSiguienteFoto();
                image.Isok =SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus;
                image.Timer =DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmss");
                ProductImages.Add(image);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                image.Isok = false;
                ProductImages.Add(image);
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas,$"FlowChart13 Error:{ex}",true);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            //finally
            //{
            //    OB_CamLight.Off();
            //}
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart14_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                  MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                  MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                  MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                  IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                  IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                OB_CamLight.Off();
                string[] pos;
                string ScanPosName = "";
                if (SysPara.OrderIndex == 2)
                {
                    ScanPosName = "PosName" + GetOrder231Index(SysPara.screwIndex.ToString());
                }
                else if (SysPara.OrderIndex == 3)
                {
                    ScanPosName = "PosName" + GetOrder312Index(SysPara.screwIndex.ToString());
                }
                else
                {
                    ScanPosName = "PosName" + SysPara.screwIndex.ToString();
                }
                pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, ScanPosName);
                posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);


                //for (int i = 0; i < dgv_AssemblyScrewPos.myDgv.Rows.Count; i++)
                //{
                //    pos = dgv_AssemblyScrewPos.GetDataArrayByIndex(i);
                //    if (Convert.ToInt32(pos[0]) == SysPara.screwIndex)
                //    {
                //        posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                //        posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                //        posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                //        break;
                //    }
                //}

                double limitZ = (double)MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("MSet", "ZLimit");
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, string.Format("Current Z valve of the target position is {0},software limit value of the z-axis is {1}", posZ, limitZ), true);
                if (posZ > limitZ)
                {
                    SDKKernal.ShowAlarm("1645");
                    DelayMs(500);
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }

                int screwCount = SysPara.screwIndex - 1;
                if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                    screwCount = screwCount - 18;
                double tempPosx, tempPosy;
                tempPosx = cacheTightenPos.ToArray()[screwCount].X;
                tempPosy = cacheTightenPos.ToArray()[screwCount].Y;


                if (IsDryCycleMode)
                    posZ = 0;

                motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = posZ;

                if ((SysPara.screwIndex - 1) % 3 == 0)
                {
                    int productCount = SysPara.screwIndex / 3;
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, productCount.ToString() + "#  Product Start Screwing ", true);

                }

                string str = (tempPosx - posX).ToString("F3") + "_" + (tempPosy - posY).ToString("F3");

                if (tempPosx == -999.999 && tempPosy == -999.999 && !IsDryCycleMode)
                {
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, " Visual inspection no product,no lock screw!", true);
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }


                if (Math.Abs(tempPosx - posX) > 2 || Math.Abs(tempPosy - posY) > 2)
                {
                    motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
                    motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "Visual feedback deviation exceeds the limit, go to the original point:GantryFlow", true);
                }
                else
                {
                    motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
                    motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(tempPosx, tempPosy, posZ);
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "The axis moves to the screw position visual offset coordinate:GantryFlow", true);

                }
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// 
        /// </summary>
        /// <param name="sender"></param>
        /// <param name="e"></param>
        /// <returns></returns>
        public string GetOrder231Index(string str)
        {
            int index = Convert.ToInt32(str);
            if (index % 3 == 0)
            {
                index = index - 2;
            }
            else
            {
                index = index + 1;
            }
            return index.ToString();
        }

        public string GetOrder312Index(string str)
        {
            int index = Convert.ToInt32(str);
            if (index % 3 == 1)
            {
                index = index + 2;
            }
            else
            {
                index = index - 1;
            }
            return index.ToString();
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart15_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The Axis has moved to the screw position:GantryFlow", true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }
        Stopwatch stopwatch = new Stopwatch();
        private FCResultType flowChart21_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            dischargecounting = 0;//20241029
            cy_ScrewLockingCylinderunite.Off();
            if (IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.Off() && IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.On())
            {
                double pickTime = 0;
                stopwatch.Stop();
                pickTime = (double)stopwatch.ElapsedMilliseconds / 1000.00;
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox2.Text = pickTime.ToString("f3");
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                count = 0;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(5000))
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1524");

                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }


        private FCResultType flowChart17_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.screwIndex += 1;
            int screwCount = 0;
            int screwIndex = SysPara.screwIndex - 1;
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
            {

                screwCount = 19;
            }
            else
            {
                screwIndex = SysPara.screwIndex - 19;
                screwCount = 37;
            }



            if (SysPara.screwIndex >= screwCount)
            {
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            if (cacheTightenPos.ToArray()[screwIndex].X != -999.999 || IsDryCycleMode)
            {
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.Off();
                if (!bScrewFeederStartEvent)
                    screwFeederStartEvent.Set();
            }


            //int ngIndex = SysPara.screwIndex / 3;
            //SysPara.screwNGIndex.Enqueue(ngIndex);

            OB_ScrewApp1.Off();//0328lhj

            //selectProgram 2
            npOutput2.On();

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart18_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart19_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (!axisAutoFlowTime.On(300))
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

            if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(5000))
            {
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.On();
            }
            SysPara.CacheScrewCount = SysPara.screwIndex;
            if (IsDryCycleMode)
            {
                processData.UpdateCount(true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            int screwIndex = SysPara.screwIndex % 3;
            if (screwIndex == 0)
                screwIndex = 3;

            if (IB_ScrewFasteningOK.On() && IB_ScrewFasteningNG.Off())
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, screwIndex.ToString() + "#  Screw OK ", true);

                processData.UpdateCount(true);
                SysPara.AllCount++;
                if (SysPara.NGContinuousCount < 3)
                {
                    SysPara.NGContinuousCount = 0;
                }
                SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG = true;
                //if (SysPara.screwIndex == 3 || SysPara.screwIndex == 6 || SysPara.screwIndex == 9
                //  || SysPara.screwIndex == 12 || SysPara.screwIndex == 15 || SysPara.screwIndex == 18 ||
                //  SysPara.screwIndex == 21 || SysPara.screwIndex == 24 || SysPara.screwIndex == 27
                //  || SysPara.screwIndex == 30 || SysPara.screwIndex == 33 || SysPara.screwIndex == 36)
                //{

                //    // processData.fnAddCountUnit(true);
                //}
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.Off();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (IB_ScrewFasteningOK.Off() && IB_ScrewFasteningNG.On())
            {

                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, screwIndex.ToString() + "#  Screw NG ", true);

                processData.UpdateCount(false);
                SysPara.NGContinuousCount++;
                SysPara.NGCount++;
                SysPara.AllCount++;
                SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG = false;
                if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewByPass"))
                {
                    axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                if (SysPara.screwIndex <= 3)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 3;
                }
                else if (3 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 6)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 6;
                }
                else if (6 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 9)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 9;
                }
                else if (9 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 12)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 12;
                }
                else if (12 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 15)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 15;
                }
                else if (15 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 18)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 18;
                }

                else if (18 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 21)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 21;
                }
                else if (21 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 24)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 24;
                }
                else if (24 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 27)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 27;
                }
                else if (27 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 30)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 30;
                }
                else if (30 < SysPara.screwIndex && SysPara.screwIndex <= 33)
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 33;
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 36;
                }

                processData.fnAddCountUnit(false);
                int ngIndex = SysPara.screwIndex / 3;
                SysPara.screwNGIndex.Enqueue(ngIndex);
                bScrewResetFlow = false;
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.Off();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            bScrewResetFlow = false;
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
        int dischargecounting = 0;

        private FCResultType flowChart46_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //20241029
            if (dischargecounting > 2)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70112");
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Discharging too many screws, please check.", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    dischargecounting = 0;
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            dischargecounting++;

            string[] pos;
            pos = dgv_NgPos.GetDataArrayByIndex(24);
            posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
            posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
            posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
            MiddleLayer.bAlignScrew = false;
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart63_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                screwFeederResetEvent.Set();
                waitMoveNGPosEvent.Set();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart64_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (screwResetOverEvent.WaitOne(0))
            {
                if (FistMoveNGPosEvent.WaitOne(0))
                {
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "After the screw is thrown, go back to the process of waiting for the tray to be in place:GantryFlow", true);
                    return FCResultType.CASE3;
                }
                else if (bScrewResetFlow)
                {
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "After the screw is thrown, go back to the screw feeder feeding process:GantryFlow", true);
                    bScrewResetFlow = false;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }

                else
                {
                    if (SysPara.screwIndex >= 18)
                    {
                        SysPara.screwIndex = 0;
                        //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "After the screw is thrown, return to the module and return to the safe position.:GantryFlow", true);
                        return FCResultType.CASE2;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart65_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (screwStartOverEvent.WaitOne(0))
            {
                OB_ScrewVacuum.On();
                OB_SuctionScrewCy.On();
                //OB_ScrewDriverStart.On();

                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Screw feeding completed:GantryFlow", true);
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            if (moveNGPosEvent.WaitOne(0))
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The screw supply fails, throw the screw:GantryFlow", true);
                //return FCResultType.NEXT;
                bScrewResetFlow = true;
                //return FCResultType.NEXT;
                return FCResultType.CASE2;
            }

            return FCResultType.IDLE;

        }
        private FCResultType flowChart20_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //if (!axisAutoFlowTime.On(100))
            //    return FCResultType.IDLE;

            if (IsDryCycleMode)
            {
                OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                npOutput2.Off();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                stopwatch.Restart();
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox2.Text = "0";
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if ((IB_ScrewVacuum.On()) || MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewByPass"))
            {
                //if ((IB_ScrewFasteningOK.On()) || IB_ScrewFasteningNG.On())
                //{
                bScrewFeederStartEvent = false;
                ScrewFeederNGNum = 0;
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.Off();//0328lhj
                npOutput2.Off();
                OB_ScrewApp1.On();

                SysPara.CurrentCount++;
                DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                dt.Rows[0]["CurrentCount"] = SysPara.CurrentCount;
                dt.AcceptChanges();
                GuardarContador();   //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v2.1.0 opcion 2 (antes WriteRecipeData aqui)
                MiddleLayer.bAlignScrew = false;
                stopwatch.Restart();
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.processData.textBox2.Text = "0";
                return FCResultType.NEXT;

            }
            else
            {
                
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(2000))
                {
                    if (!MiddleLayer.bAlignScrew)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.bAlignScrew = true;
                        return FCResultType.CASE1;
                    }
                    if (ScrewFeederNGNum > 2)
                    {
                        ScrewFeederNGNum = 0;
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1702");
                    }
                    ScrewFeederNGNum++;
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The screw vacuum does not reach the standard value, go to throw the screw", true);
                    bScrewResetFlow = true;
                    bScrewFeederStartEvent = false;
                    //OB_ScrewFeederStart.Off();
                    //DelayMs(500);
                    //OB_ScrewFeederStart.On();
                    SysPara.CurrentCount++;
                    DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
                    dt.Rows[0]["CurrentCount"] = SysPara.CurrentCount;
                    dt.AcceptChanges();
                    GuardarContador();   //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v2.1.0 opcion 2 (antes WriteRecipeData aqui)

                    return FCResultType.CASE2;
                }

            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;

        }
        int count = 0;
        private FCResultType flowChart23_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //Thread.Sleep(50);
            if (IB_ScrewDriverReady.On())
            {
                mTF6000.bCurveAngle = false;
                mTF6000.bCurveTorques = false;
                mTF6000.bScrewResult = false;



                OB_ScrewDriverStart.On();
                //screwFeederStartEvent.Set();
                //OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(2000))
                {
                    count++;
                    OB_ScrewApp1.Off();
                    DelayMs(200);
                    OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                    axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                    if (count == 2)
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1703");
                    }

                }
            }

            //}
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart25_FlowRun_1(object sender,EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                screwTimeoutTimer.Restart();//----------------------------------------找到上一个FlowChat-------------------------------------
                // Dry Run
                if (IsDryCycleMode)
                {
                    SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque = "12.5";
                    SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle = "1200";
                    SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalResultString = "True";
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
                // Torque Retry Logic
                if (mTF6000.Torques > 50)
                {
                    if (getScrewResultNumber < 3)
                    {
                        getScrewResultNumber++;
                        mTF6000.bScrewResult = false;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        mTF6000.bScrewResult = true;
                    }
                }

                // Result Ready
                if (mTF6000.bScrewResult &&mTF6000.bCurveAngle &&mTF6000.bCurveTorques)
                {
                    RemainingTimes++;
                    // Time Data
                    List<double> time = new List<double>();
                    double currentTime = 0;
                    for (int i = 0; i < mTF6000.CurveTorques.Count; i++)
                    {
                        currentTime += 0.003;
                        time.Add(currentTime);
                    }

                    // Screw Index
                    int screwIndex = (SysPara.screwIndex - 1) / 3;
                    if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond)
                    {
                        screwIndex -= 6;
                    }

                    // Index Protection
                    if (screwIndex < 0 ||screwIndex >= 6)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas, $"ScrewIndex Error:{screwIndex}",true);
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }

                    string qrcode = SysPara.TempPalletCode[screwIndex];
                    int screwCount = (SysPara.screwIndex - 1) % 3 + 1;

                    try
                    {
                        string[] code = MiddleLayer.ConveyorF .UpConveyorAutoFlow .BFirstOrSecond?new string[]
                            {
                                PCBCode[0],
                                PCBCode[1],
                                PCBCode[2],
                                PCBCode[6],
                                PCBCode[7],
                                PCBCode[8]
                            }
                            :
                            new string[]
                            {
                                PCBCode[3],
                                PCBCode[4],
                                PCBCode[5],
                                PCBCode[9],
                                PCBCode[10],
                                PCBCode[11]
                            };

                        string pcbCode = code[screwIndex];

                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(pcbCode))
                        {
                            pcbCode = "NA";
                        }

                        string path =MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet","SaveImagePath")+ "\\";
                        Directory.CreateDirectory(path);
                        string imageName =string.Format( @"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.png", pcbCode,qrcode, DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfff"),MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID"));

                        //Task.Run(() =>
                        //{
                        //    try
                        //    {
                        //        ShowImage( mTF6000.CurveTorques, mTF6000.CurveAngles,time, screwCount.ToString(),qrcode,mTF6000.Torques,mTF6000.Angles,path + imageName);
                        //    }
                        //    catch (Exception ex)
                        //    {
                        //        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas,$"ShowImage Error:{ex}",true);
                        //    }
                        //});
                        var torques = new List<double>(mTF6000.CurveTorques);
                        var angles = new List<double>(mTF6000.CurveAngles);
                        var times2 = new List<double>(time);

                        double torque = mTF6000.Torques;
                        double angle = mTF6000.Angles;

                        Task.Run(() =>
                        {
                            ShowImage(torques,angles,times2,screwCount.ToString(),qrcode,torque,angle,path + imageName);
                        });
                    }
                    catch (Exception ex)
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas,$"Save Curve Error:{ex}",true);
                    }

                    // Save Result
                    SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle =mTF6000.Angles.ToString();
                    SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque =mTF6000.Torques.ToString();
                    SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalResultString =SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG.ToString();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }

                // Wait Result
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(300))
                {
                    if (!mTF6000.bScrewResult)
                    {
                        mTF6000.GetScrewResult(3);

                        axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();

                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }

                    if (!mTF6000.bCurveAngle)
                    {
                        mTF6000.GetScrewResult(1);

                        axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();

                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }

                    if (!mTF6000.bCurveTorques)
                    {
                        mTF6000.GetScrewResult(2);

                        axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();

                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }

                // IMPORTANT:
                // Prevent infinite wait
                if (screwTimeoutTimer.On(5000))
                {
                    screwTimeoutTimer.Restart();
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas, "TF6000 Result Timeout",true);
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1206");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }

                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas, $"flowChart25 Error:{ex}", true);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1206");
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart29_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int screwCount = 18;
            if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                screwCount = 36;

            if (SysPara.screwIndex >= screwCount)
            {

                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart33_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
            {
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.cy_TransverseCylinderInunite.Off();
                if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TransverseCylinderOut.Off(200) && MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TransverseCylinderIn.On(200))
                {
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(5000))
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1015");
                    }
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.cy_TransverseCylinderInunite.On();
                if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TransverseCylinderOut.On(200) && MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TransverseCylinderIn.Off(200))
                {
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(5000))
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1015");
                    }
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }


        }
        private FCResultType flowChart34_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {//bEnableMasterSampleModel
            bool bEnableMasterSampleModel = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "bEnableMasterSampleModel");
            if (bEnableMasterSampleModel)
            {
                bool enableCheckPalletCount = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableCheckPalletCount");
                if (enableCheckPalletCount)
                {
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The automatic process is completed in a single cycle:GantryFlow", true);
                    int index = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                    if ((int)processData.nPalletTotalUnits >= index)
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0009");

                    }
                }
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true; //SysPara.R1AssembCompeletStatus = true; lhj_0512
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Clear();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                processData.UpdatePalletCount();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (SysPara.screwNGIndex.Count > 0)
            {


                SysPara.R1AssembResult = false;

                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            else
            {
                bool enableCheckPalletCount = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableCheckPalletCount");
                if (enableCheckPalletCount)
                {
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The automatic process is completed in a single cycle:GantryFlow", true);
                    int index = GetSettingValue("MSet", "ProQuantity");
                    if ((int)processData.nPalletTotalUnits >= index)
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0009");

                    }
                }
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true; //SysPara.R1AssembCompeletStatus = true; lhj_0512
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Clear();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                processData.UpdatePalletCount();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart37_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                NGIndex = SysPara.screwNGIndex.Dequeue();
                string[] pos;
                string ScanPosName = "FlowNGPos" + NGIndex;
                pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_Calibration, ScanPosName);
                posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);


                //for (int i = 0; i < dgv_Calibration.myDgv.Rows.Count; i++)
                //{
                //    pos = dgv_Calibration.GetDataArrayByIndex(i);
                //    if (Convert.ToInt32(pos[0]) == NGIndex)
                //    {
                //        posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);

                //        posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                //        break;
                //    }
                //}
                motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
                motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
                string str = NGIndex.ToString() + "_" + posX.ToString() + "_" + posY.ToString() + "_" + posZ.ToString();
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "The axis starts to move to the NG take-up position:GantryFlow", true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart42_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                           MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                           MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                           MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                           IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                           IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                int index = GetSettingValue("MSet", "NGPosIndex");
                if (index > 24)
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0006");
                    index = 1;
                }
                string[] pos;
                for (int i = 0; i < dgv_NgPos.myDgv.Rows.Count; i++)
                {
                    pos = dgv_NgPos.GetDataArrayByIndex(i);
                    if (Convert.ToInt32(pos[0]) == index)
                    {
                        posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                        posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                        posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
                motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
                string str = index.ToString() + "_" + posX.ToString() + "_" + posY.ToString() + "_" + posZ.ToString();
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "The axis starts to move to the discharge position:GantryFlow", true);
                index++;
                DataTable dt = SettingData.Tables["MSet"];
                dt.Rows[0]["NGPosIndex"] = index;
                dt.AcceptChanges();
                this.WriteSettingData();




                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart43_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The axis has moved to the discharge position:GantryFlow", true);
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();

                //Thread.Sleep(300);
                DelayMs(300);
                if (IB_TrayInPlace.Off(100))
                {
                    int index = GetSettingValue("MSet", "NGPosIndex");
                    if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                        NGIndex = NGIndex - 6;
                    string QRCode = SysPara.TempPalletCode[NGIndex - 1];
                    string str = string.Format(NGIndex.ToString() + "# Product,QRCode:{0},NGIndex:" + (index - 1).ToString(), QRCode);
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str, true);
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.NGProduct, str, true);

                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {



                    return FCResultType.CASE1;

                }



                //     return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart68_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            mTF6000.IP = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScrewIP");
            mTF6000.Port = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScrewPort");
            if (!mTF6000.ConnectStatus)
            {
                mTF6000.Atlas_Connect();
            }


            if (mTF6000.ConnectStatus)
            {
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(lbl_ScrewStaus, () =>
                { lbl_ScrewStaus.BackColor = Color.Green; });
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Connect the electric batch successfully", true);
                Gantry1.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(lbl_ScrewStaus, () =>
                { lbl_ScrewStaus.BackColor = Color.Red; });
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1300");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart76_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //double HighSpeed = GetSettingValue("MSet", "AxisZ_HomeHighSpd");
            //double LowSpeed = GetSettingValue("MSet", "AxisZ_HomeLowSpd");
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.servo.manualReset.Set();

            //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - reinicio rapido: sin home si los tres ejes conservan la referencia
            //  Cilindros y desarmador ya subieron en flowChart69/66/70. Si hace falta home,
            //  IniciarMovimientoSeguroSinHome devuelve false y sigue el home del proveedor.
            bRestartQuickSinHome = false;
            if (bRestartQuickActive && IniciarMovimientoSeguroSinHome())
            {
                InitJT.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            double HighSpeed = 5;
            double LowSpeed = 3;
            int timeOut = GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut");
            servo.HomeAxis(ServoAixsName.Z, HighSpeed, LowSpeed, timeOut);
            //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The Z axis starts to return to the origin position", true);
            InitJT.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart77_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - reinicio rapido sin home: esperar llegada a 0,0,0
            //  Si no llega, UC_MotorMove da sus propias alarmas de tiempo (0021/0022/0023).
            if (bRestartQuickSinHome)
            {
                if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
                {
                    //  Lo que sigue (tirar pieza a NG si el gripper trae una) va a velocidad normal,
                    //  igual que en la inicializacion completa. npFlowChart1 vuelve a aplicar
                    //  AxisParaSetting al final.
                    AjustarVelocidadEjes(1.0);
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production,
                        "Reinicio rapido: ejes en 0,0,0 sin home.", true);
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            if (servo.Servo_Z_IsHomeOK)
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Z axis return to origin position completed", true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (InitJT.On(180000))
                {
                    InitJT.Restart();
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1415");
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

                else
                {

                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }



            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart101_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int index = GetSettingValue("MSet", "NGPosIndex");
            if (index > 24) { index = 1; }
            string[] pos;
            for (int i = 0; i < dgv_NgPos.myDgv.Rows.Count; i++)
            {
                pos = dgv_NgPos.GetDataArrayByIndex(i);
                if (Convert.ToInt32(pos[0]) == index)
                {
                    posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                    posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                    posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                    break;
                }
            }
            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
            string str = index.ToString() + "_" + posX.ToString() + "_" + posY.ToString() + "_" + posZ.ToString();
            //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "Notify the axis to move to the NG discharge position :GantryFlow", true);
            index++;
            DataTable dt = SettingData.Tables["MSet"];
            dt.Rows[0]["NGPosIndex"] = index;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            this.WriteSettingData();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart61_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The axis has moved to the discharge position :GantryFlow", true);
                Gantry1.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart89_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)

        }


        private FCResultType flowChart90_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (screwFeederStartEvent.WaitOne(0))
            {
                if (IsDryCycleMode)
                {
                    screwStartOverEvent.Set();
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Screw machine starts feeding:ScrewFeeder", true);
                screwFeederAutoTime.Restart();
                bScrewFeederStartEvent = true;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            if (screwFeederResetEvent.WaitOne(0))
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The screw machine starts to throw the material:ScrewFeeder", true);
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }

            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart88_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //if (screwFeederAutoTime.On(200))
            //    return FCResultType.IDLE;
            //if (MiddleLayer.GantryF.IB_ScrewFeederShortage.On())
            //{

            //    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1707");
            //}
            if (IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.On())
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1705");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            if (!MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewByPass"))
            {
                OB_ScrewFeederStart.On();
            }
            //MoveTime.Restart();
            Task t = new Task(() =>
            {
                ScrewTime.Restart();
                while (true)
                {
                    if (IB_ScrewRing.On() || MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewByPass"))
                    {

                        magnetRingEvent.Set();
                        return;
                    }
                    if (ScrewTime.On(5000))
                        return;
                }
            });
            t.Start();

            screwFeederAutoTime.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;

        }

        private FCResultType flowChart87_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.On())
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1705");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            if (true || MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewByPass"))//IB_ScrewFeederSpike.On() ||
            {
                magnetRingEvent.Reset();
                OB_ScrewFeederStart.Off();
                screwFeederAutoTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (screwFeederAutoTime.On(5000))
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1706");

            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart85_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (magnetRingEvent.WaitOne(0) || MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewByPass"))
            {
                ScrewFeederNGNum = 0;
                OB_ScrewFeederStart.Off();
                screwFeederAutoTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (screwFeederAutoTime.On(6000))
                {
                    if (ScrewFeederNGNum > 2)
                    {
                        ScrewFeederNGNum = 0;
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1702");
                    }
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Ring sensor feeding failure", true);
                    ScrewFeederNGNum++;
                    OB_ScrewFeederStart.Off();
                    DelayMs(500);
                    OB_ScrewFeederStart.On();
                    return FCResultType.CASE2;
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart93_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.Off() && IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.On())
            {
                if (IB_SuctionScrewCy.On(100) && screwFeederAutoTime.On(100))
                {
                    cy_ScrewLockingCylinderunite.On();
                    OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
                    screwFeederAutoTime.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (screwFeederAutoTime.On(5000))
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1524");
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
        private FCResultType flowChart48_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                if (IB_SuctionScrewCy.Off())
                {
                    screwFeederResetEvent.Reset();
                    screwResetOverEvent.Set();
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "Screw machine feeding completed:ScrewFeeder", true);
                    screwFeederStartEvent.Reset();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (screwFeederAutoTime.On(5000))
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1523");
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        public ManualResetEvent motorMoveEnable1 = new ManualResetEvent(true);
        private void btn_TestRun_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (btn_TestRun.BackColor == SystemColors.Control)
                btn_TestRun.BackColor = Color.Green;

            MiddleLayer.GantryF.motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut");
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.motorMoveCtrl.motorMoveEnable.Set();
            fc_Transform.TaskReset();
            posIndex = 0;
            _bmoveComplete = false;
            Task.Factory.StartNew(() =>
            {
                while (!_bmoveComplete)
                {
                    motorMoveEnable1.WaitOne();
                    fc_Transform.TaskRun();
                }
            });
            motorMoveEnable1.Set();
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.servo.manualReset.Set();
        }

        private void btn_Stop_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            _bmoveComplete = true;
            motorMoveEnable1.Reset();
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.servo.manualReset.Reset();
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.motorMoveCtrl.motorMoveEnable.Reset();
            MiddleLayer.GantryF.motorMoveCtrl.BautoRun = false;
            MiddleLayer.StopAllMotor();
        }

        private void btnConnectScrew_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            mTF6000.IP = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScrewIP");
            mTF6000.Port = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ScrewPort");
            if (!mTF6000.ConnectStatus)
            {
                mTF6000.Atlas_Connect();
            }
            mTF6000.bResultSet = true;
            if (mTF6000.ConnectStatus)
            {
                lbl_ScrewStaus.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else
            {
                lbl_ScrewStaus.BackColor = Color.Red;
            }

        }

        private void btnDisScrew_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            mTF6000.Disconnect();

            lbl_ScrewStaus.BackColor = Color.Red;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart22_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 1)
                NowProductQR = SysPara.TempPalletCode.ToArray()[0];
            else if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount > 3)
            {
                float bQR = (SysPara.CacheScrewCount - 1) % 3;
                if (bQR == 0)
                {
                    int index = (SysPara.CacheScrewCount - 1) / 3;
                    if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                        index = index - 6;
                    NowProductQR = SysPara.TempPalletCode.ToArray()[index];
                }
            }
            ScrewTighteningData screwTighteningData = new ScrewTighteningData();
            screwTighteningData.Time = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss");
            screwTighteningData.MatCode = NowProductQR;
            screwTighteningData.Point = SysPara.CacheScrewCount;
            if (SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque == "")
            {
                screwTighteningData.Torque = Convert.ToDouble(12.044);
                screwTighteningData.Angle = Convert.ToDouble(1232);
            }
            else
            {
                screwTighteningData.Torque = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque);
                screwTighteningData.Angle = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle);
            }
            //screwTighteningData.Result = SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalStatus ? "OK" : "NG";
            screwTighteningData.Result = SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG ? "OK" : "NG";
            #region
            //if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 1)
            //{
            //    SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree = screwTighteningData.Torque.ToString();
            //    SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree = screwTighteningData.Angle.ToString();
            //}
            //else if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 2)
            //{
            //    SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree += "-"+ screwTighteningData.Torque.ToString();
            //    SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree += "-" + screwTighteningData.Angle.ToString();
            //}
            //else if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 3)
            //{
            //    SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree += "-" + screwTighteningData.Torque.ToString();
            //    SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree += "-"+ screwTighteningData.Angle.ToString();

            //    try
            //    {
            //        if (screwTighteningData.Result == "OK")
            //        {
            //            MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(screwTighteningData.MatCode, SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree, SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree);
            //        }
            //        else
            //        {
            //            MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(screwTighteningData.MatCode, "Fail-Fail-Fail", "Fail-Fail-Fail");
            //        }
            //        SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree = "";
            //        SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree = "";

            //    }
            //    catch (Exception)
            //    {

            //        throw;
            //    }
            //}
            #endregion
            //processData.UpdateResult(screwTighteningData);
            bool bFirst = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");
            processData.UpdateResult(screwTighteningData, CurrentRowsIndex, bFirst);
            CurrentRowsIndex++;
            // add one row at the up part, 2023.1.27, zenan
            int maxrows = Convert.ToInt32(MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "MaxDisplay"));
            if (maxrows <= 0)
            {
                maxrows = 12;
            }
            if (processData.fnRowsCount() > maxrows)
            {
                processData.fnRemoveLastResult();
            }

            SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Enqueue(screwTighteningData);

            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Count == 1)
            {
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle).ToString();
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque = (Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque) * 100000).ToString();
            }
            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Count == 2)
            {
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle += "-" + Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle).ToString();
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque += "-" + (Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque) * 100000).ToString();
            }
            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Count == 3 || screwTighteningData.Result == "NG")
            {
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle += "-" + Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle).ToString();
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque += "-" + (Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque) * 100000).ToString();
                if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.bMESEnabled)
                {
                    if (screwTighteningData.Result == "NG")
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(NowProductQR, "Fail-Fail-Fail", "Fail-Fail-Fail");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(NowProductQR, SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle, SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque);
                    }
                    WriteMes(NowProductQR, SysPara.screwTightrningDatas);
                }

                //SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Clear();
            }
            axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart1_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            AxisParaSetting();
            bInitialOk = true;
            //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - fin del reinicio rapido
            //  Se apagan las banderas para que una inicializacion normal no salte nada.
            //  Este nodo se repite hasta que FlowControl cierra la inicializacion; el log sale una vez.
            if (bRestartQuickActive)
            {
                bRestartQuickActive = false;
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production,
                    bRestartQuickSinHome
                        ? "Reinicio rapido: terminado sin home, ejes en 0,0,0."
                        : "Reinicio rapido: terminado con home completo.",
                    true);
            }
            bRestartQuickSkipMes = false;
            bRestartQuickSinHome = false;
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
