        private FCResultType npFlowChart4_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.bSecondRoundFlag)
            {
                SysPara.bSecondRoundFlag = false;
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.NotifyAssembly = false; //lhj_0512
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = false;//lhj_0512
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BEnableFirstGroup = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BEnableSecondGroup = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSecondGroup");
            }
            // clear mes variables

            //for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs.Length; i++)
            //{
            //    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[i] = "";
            //}
            mTF6000.AngleCount = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "AngleCount");
            mTF6000.TorquesCount = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "TorqueCount");
            mTF6000.bResultSet = false;



            SysPara.CountLimit = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "CountLimit");
            SysPara.CurrentCount = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "CurrentCount");
            if (SysPara.CurrentCount > SysPara.CountLimit)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1307", "Screws are almost out,please check!");
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Screws are almost out,please check!,yes or no?", true, false, false);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    RemainingTimes = 0;
                }

            }

            if (SysPara.NGCount >= SysPara.NGCountLimit || SysPara.AllCount % SysPara.OKCountLimit == 0)
            {
                if (SysPara.NGCount >= SysPara.NGCountLimit)
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("50033", "So many NG,please check!");
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("So many NG,please check!,yes or no?", true, false, false);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {
                        SysPara.NGCount = 0;
                        SysPara.AllCount = 0;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.AllCount = 0;
                    SysPara.NGCount = 0;
                }
            }

            if (SysPara.NGContinuousCount >= SysPara.NGContinuousCountLimit)
            {
                if (SysPara.NGContinuousCount >= SysPara.NGContinuousCountLimit)
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("50032", "Consecutive NG,please check!");
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("So many NG,please check!,yes or no?", true, false, false);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {
                        SysPara.NGContinuousCount = 0;
                    }
                }
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
            {
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs[i] = false;
            }

            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = false;
            string ScanPosName = "ScanPos1";
            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, ScanPosName);
            motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = Convert.ToInt32(GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut"));
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(Convert.ToDouble(pos[2]), Convert.ToDouble(pos[3]), Convert.ToDouble(pos[4]));
            string str = pos[2] + "_" + pos[3] + "_" + pos[4];
            //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "The axis moves to the scanning position of the conveyor tray:GantryFlow", true);

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        }

        private void tm_GantryWork_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "dcEnableCheckProcess"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0008");
            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0008");
            }
            if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "IsDryCycleMode"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0011");
            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0011");
            }
            if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableVision"))
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("0014");
            }
            else
            {
                SDKKernal.ClearAlarm("0014");
            }
        }

        private void button1_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (mTF6000.ConnectStatus)
            {
                //mTF6000.GetScrewResult(2);

                mTF6000.bCurveTorques = false;

                DelayMs(300);
                mTF6000.GetScrewResult(2);
                //Thread.Sleep(300);
                DelayMs(300);
                mTF6000.GetScrewResult(3);
                //Thread.Sleep(300);
                DelayMs(300);
                List<double> time = new List<double>();
                string path = @"E:\新建文件夹\123.png";
                DateTime timeBase = new DateTime(2020, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0);
                double timeCoefficientOA = timeBase.AddSeconds(0.003).ToOADate() - timeBase.ToOADate();
                double TimeD = 0;

                for (int i = 0; i < 5000; i++)
                {
                    TimeD += timeCoefficientOA;
                    time.Add(TimeD);
                }
                if (mTF6000.bCurveAngle && mTF6000.bCurveTorques)
                {
                    ShowImage(mTF6000.CurveTorques, mTF6000.CurveAngles, time, "0", "123456", mTF6000.Torques, mTF6000.Angles, path);
                }
                else
                {
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Get Curev Data Fill", true, false, true);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {

                    }
                }

            }




        }

        private FCResultType flowChart18_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IsDryCycleMode)
            {
                SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.screwTightrningData;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            if (RemainingTimes >= MaxBatchLife)
            {
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1301", "The electric batch has reached the end of its service life,Please replace the batch head");
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("The electric batch has reached the end of its service life,Please replace the batch head,yes or no?", true, false, false);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {
                    RemainingTimes = 0;
                }

            }
            DataTable dt = RecipeData.Tables["RSet"];
            dt.Rows[0]["RemainingTimes"] = RemainingTimes;
            dt.AcceptChanges();
            WriteRecipeData(string.Format("{0}\\{1}.xml", SysPara.RecipeDataDirectory, SysPara.RecipeName));

            // check error
            try
            {
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo($"angle, {SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle},torque, {SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque}, resultstring, {SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalResultString}, status, {SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalStatus}, result, {SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG}");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo($"valu get error, {ex.Message}");
            }
            try
            {
                double angle = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle);
                double tor = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque);
                if (((tor < 10 || tor > 14) || (angle < 0 || angle > 2000)) &&
                    SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG)
                {
                    int ngIndex = SysPara.screwIndex / 3;
                    int delta = SysPara.screwIndex % 3;
                    if (delta > 0)
                        ngIndex++;
                    MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo($"ScrewIndex is {SysPara.screwIndex}, ngIndex is {ngIndex}");
                    SysPara.screwNGIndex.Enqueue(ngIndex);
                    processData.fnAddCountUnit(false);
                    SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG = false;
                    MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo($"Double check NG");
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70111");
                }
                else
                {
                    if (SysPara.screwIndex == 3 || SysPara.screwIndex == 6 || SysPara.screwIndex == 9
                       || SysPara.screwIndex == 12 || SysPara.screwIndex == 15 || SysPara.screwIndex == 18 ||
                          SysPara.screwIndex == 21 || SysPara.screwIndex == 24 || SysPara.screwIndex == 27
                       || SysPara.screwIndex == 30 || SysPara.screwIndex == 33 || SysPara.screwIndex == 36)
                    {

                        processData.fnAddCountUnit(true);
                    }

                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo($"valu check error, {ex.Message}");
            }


            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache == null)
            {
                SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.screwTightrningData;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if ((SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache.Value.TotalAngle == SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle) &&
                    (SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache.Value.PeekTorque == SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque))
                {
                    SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.screwTightrningData;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;












                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.screwTightrningData;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart103_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            List<ScrewTighteningData> screwTightrningDatas = new List<ScrewTighteningData>(SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.ToList());

            {
                string data = item.MatCode + "," + item.Point.ToString() + "," +

                string header = "Date" + "," + "Time" + "," + "MatCode" + "," + "Index" + "," +
                    "Torque" + "," + "Angle" + "," + "Result";

                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.SaveLogCSV(data, "ScrewTightenData", header);
            }

            //string sn = "PalletCode_" + DateTime.Now.ToString("hhmmss");
            //WriteMes(sn, SysPara.screwTightrningDatas);

            motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = -30;
            SysPara.screwIndex = 0;
            //SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Clear();
            if (SysPara.screwNGIndex.Count == 0)
                SysPara.TempPalletCode.Clear();

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private async Task WriteMes(string sSerialNumber, Queue<ScrewTighteningData> screwDatas)
        {
            MESParameter mesPara = new MESParameter();
            bool bStatusOk = true;
            try
            {
                List<ScrewTighteningData> dataList;
                lock (screwDatas)
                {
                    dataList = screwDatas.ToList();
                    screwDatas.Clear();
                }

                for (int i = 0;i < dataList.Count; i++)
                {
                    ScrewTighteningData temp = dataList[i];
                    // NG
                    if (temp.Result == "NG")
                    {
                        bStatusOk = false;
                        KeyValuePair<string, string> failData = new KeyValuePair<string, string>( $"ScrewTighteningData{i + 1}_FAIL",$"{temp.Torque},{temp.Angle}");
                        mesPara.fails.Add(failData);
                    }

                    // Measurement
                    Measurement measureData = new Measurement();
                    measureData.measureLabel = $"ScrewTighteningData{i + 1}";
                    measureData.measureData = $"{temp.Torque},{temp.Angle}";
                    measureData.measureMessage = temp.Result == "OK" ? $"ScrewTighteningData{i + 1}_PASS" : $"ScrewTighteningData{i + 1}_FAIL";
                    mesPara.measurements.Add( measureData);
                }
                string pcbSN = sSerialNumber;
                try
                {
                    PalletInfo palletInfo = cTLAPalletLink.TLA_GetPalletInfo( "http://mxgdlm0aut01/TLPalletLink/api/Public?PalletNumber=",  sSerialNumber);

                    if (palletInfo != null &&!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace( palletInfo.PCBSerialNumber))
                    {
                        pcbSN = palletInfo.PCBSerialNumber;
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas, $"Get PalletInfo Error:{ex}",true);
                }

                // IMPORTANT:
                // MES Upload
                await MiddleLayer.MesF.WriteUnitMES( pcbSN, null, null,true, bStatusOk);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas,$"WriteMES Error:{ex}",true);
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart107_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            double exposure1 = GetSettingValue("MSet", "TrayDetectExpTime");
            if (H1_TrayDetect.TakePicture(exposure1))                                       //CCD take picture
            {
                OB_CamLight.Off();
                H1_TrayDetect.RunTB();

                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(cogRecDisp, () =>
                {
                    cogRecDisp.Image = H1_TrayDetect.InputImage as CogImage8Grey;
                    cogRecDisp.Record = H1_TrayDetect.TB.CreateLastRunRecord().SubRecords[0];
                    cogRecDisp.Fit();
                });
                if (H1_TrayDetect.IsAccept)
                {
                    bool ret = (bool)H1_TrayDetect.GetOutput("InspectResult");
                    if (ret)
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                    else { return FCResultType.NEXT; }
                }
                else
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1204");
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            else
            {

                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1709");

            //        }
            //    }

            //if (MiddleLayer.SystemF.OB_RightDoorCyDown.GetState())

        private bool DectNetworkCable(string ip)
        {
            try
            {
                Ping ping = new Ping();
                PingReply p = ping.Send(ip);
                if (p.Status == IPStatus.Success)
                {
                    return true;
                }
                else
                {
                    return false;
                }
            }
            catch (Exception)
            {

                return false;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart67_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            SysPara.ReaderPort = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "ReaderPort");
            SysPara.ScanCom.Close();
            SysPara.ScanCom.Open(SysPara.ReaderPort);
            if (SysPara.Readerthread != null && SysPara.Readerthread.IsAlive)
            {
                SysPara.Readerthread.Abort();
            }
            SysPara.ScanCom.isDataNew = false;
            SysPara.Readerthread = new Thread(Receive);
            SysPara.Readerthread.IsBackground = true;
            SysPara.Readerthread.Start();
            //return FCResultType.NEXT;
            if (SysPara.ScanCom.ConnectStatus())
            {
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "HandHeld Reader connected successfully", true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }
        #region Screw feeder scan code to open the cover
        public void Receive()
        {
            while (true)
            {
                lock (SysPara.StatusLock)
                {
                    ReceiveRefresh();
                    Receive1();
                }
                //Thread.Sleep(10);
                DelayMs(10);
            }
        }
        JTimer screwFeederJT = new JTimer();
        private void ReceiveRefresh()
        {
            if (OB_ScrewFeederDoorOpen.GetState())
            {
                if (screwFeederJT.On(3000) && IB_ScrewFeederDoor.On())
                {
                    OB_ScrewFeederDoorOpen.Off();
                    if (IB_ScrewFeederDoor.Off())
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1708");
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        /// <summary>
        /// Screw feeder scan code to receive data
        /// </summary>
        public void Receive1()
        {
            if (SysPara.ScanCom.isDataNew)
            {
                string codedata = GetRecipeValue("RSet", "FeederSettingCode");
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(txt_FeederCurrentCode, () =>
                {
                    txt_FeederCurrentCode.Text = SysPara.ScanCom.ReceiveData;
                });
                if (!SysPara.ScanCom.ReceiveData.Contains("?"))
                {
                    if (SysPara.ScanCom.ReceiveData.Contains(codedata))
                    {
                        screwFeederJT.Restart();
                        OB_ScrewFeederDoorOpen.On();
                    }
                    else { SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1710"); }
                }
                SysPara.ScanCom.isDataNew = false;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        private FCResultType flowChart105_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                string[] pos;
                for (int i = 0; i < dgv_GrabPosition.myDgv.Rows.Count; i++)
                {
                    pos = dgv_GrabPosition.GetDataArrayByIndex(i);
                    if (Convert.ToInt32(pos[0]) == 10)
                    {
                        posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                        posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                        posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                        break;
                    }
                }
                motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = false;
                motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
                string str = posX.ToString() + "_" + posY.ToString() + "_" + posZ.ToString();
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "Move the axis to the photo position of the NG material tray:GantryFlow", true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private void btn_Home_Click1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string btnName = ((Button)sender).Name;
            string flagStr = "btn_Home";
            string axisName = btnName.Replace(flagStr, "");
            double HighSpeed = 10;//GetSettingValue("MSet", "AxisZ_HomeHighSpd");
            double LowSpeed = 5;//GetSettingValue("MSet", "AxisZ_HomeLowSpd");
            int timeOut = 50000;//GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut");
            switch (axisName)
            {
                case "X1":
                    servo.HomeAxis(ServoAixsName.X, HighSpeed, LowSpeed, timeOut);
                    break;

                case "Y":
                    servo.HomeAxis(ServoAixsName.Y, HighSpeed, LowSpeed, timeOut);
                    break;
                case "Z":
                    servo.HomeAxis(ServoAixsName.Z, HighSpeed, LowSpeed, timeOut);
                    break;
            }
        }

        int TrayCodeOffset = 0;
        private FCResultType npFlowChart5_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
            {
                TrayCodeOffset = 0;
            }
            else
            {
                TrayCodeOffset = 3;
            }
            // Products exist
            int index = 0;
            // mapping
            switch (SysPara.screwIndex)
            {
                case 1:
                case 2:
                case 3:
                case 10:
                case 11:
                case 12:
                    index = SysPara.screwIndex - 1;
                    break;
                case 4:
                case 5:
                case 6:
                    index = SysPara.screwIndex - 1 + 3;
                    break;
                case 7:
                case 8:
                case 9:
                    index = SysPara.screwIndex - 1 - 3;
                    break;
                default:
                    throw new Exception($"SysPara.screwIndex: {SysPara.screwIndex}, not supported");
            }
            //if (SysPara.screwIndex <= 3)
            //{
            //    index = SysPara.screwIndex - 1 + TrayCodeOffset;
            //}
            //else
            //{
            //    index = SysPara.screwIndex - 1 + 3 + TrayCodeOffset;
            //}
            MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs[index]
                = SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus;

            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo(
                string.Format("screwIndex = {0}, index = {1}, status = {2}",
                SysPara.screwIndex, index, SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus));

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        {
            //bool bFirst = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");

            SysPara.EnableMes = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "Enable_Mes");
            MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes = SysPara.EnableMes;
            if (!MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesInitE)
            {
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesInit();
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesInitE = true;
            }
            string EQPName = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPName");
            MESLib.CommParas.MesManager._connector.MDLN = EQPName;

            MESLib.CommParas.IP = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "Mes_IP");
            MESLib.CommParas.Port = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "Mes_Port");   // secgem
            //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - reinicio rapido: no reconectar SECS/GEM
            //  Reconectar hace que el host mande IDLE REASON / OFFLINE REASON y OP CALL.
            if (bRestartQuickSkipMes) { return FCResultType.NEXT; }
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                // secgem
                MESLib.CommFuns.StartSecsGem(out string error);
                if (error != string.Empty)
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70105"); // secgem open fail
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.PauseRunning();
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
                //System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(1500);
                DelayMs(1200);
                if (!MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesConnect(MESLib.CommParas.IP, MESLib.CommParas.Port))
                {
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70109"); // secgem open fail
                    //System.Threading.Thread.Sleep(1000);
                    //MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MesClose();
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }

            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart11_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            bool bFirst = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");
            bool bSecond = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSecondGroup");
            // clear 10711 information
            if (!bFirst || !bSecond)
            {
                bFirstEmpty = false;
                bSecondEmpty = false;

                for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
                {
                    Position1Data[i] = new ScrewTighteningData();
                    Position2Data[i] = new ScrewTighteningData();
                    Position3Data[i] = new ScrewTighteningData();

                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[i] = "";
                }
            }
            else if (bFirst && bSecond && MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond)//SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
            {
                bFirstEmpty = false;
                bSecondEmpty = false;

                for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++)
                {
                    Position1Data[i] = new ScrewTighteningData();
                    Position2Data[i] = new ScrewTighteningData();
                    Position3Data[i] = new ScrewTighteningData();

                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[i] = "";
                }
            }

            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
            {
                TrayCodeOffset = 0;
            }
            else
            {
                TrayCodeOffset = 3;
            }
            // Tray QRCodes
            for (int i = 0; i < SysPara.TempPalletCode.Count; i++)
            {
                int index = 0;
                if (i < 3)
                {
                    index = i + TrayCodeOffset;
                }
                else
                {
                    index = i + 3 + TrayCodeOffset;
                }
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index]
                    = SysPara.TempPalletCode[i];
            }
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Contains(true))
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }

            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs._ToString());
            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs._ToString());
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10701 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10701 = new JTimer();
        JTimer Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME = new JTimer();

        private FCResultType npFlowChart12_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                try
                {
                    //List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                    List<string> _Pocket_List = new List<string>();
                    //List<string> _Module_List = new List<string>();
                    List<string> _Module_List = new List<string>();

                    for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
                    {
                        if (!MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs[i])
                            continue;
                        int index = i;

                        _Pocket_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[index]);
                        _Module_List.Add(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index]);
                    }
                    //string _RecipeID = (string)MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "modelItem");

                    Timestamp_10701 = DateTime.Now;
                    Timer_10701.Restart();
                    Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME.Restart();
                    //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v1.0.6.5.3 producto distinto: respuesta anterior, solo lectura
                    loteStartAntesDel10701 = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_STR;
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK = false;
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK = false;

                    MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10701");
                    MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Pocket_List: " + _Pocket_List._ToString());
                    MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Module_List: " + _Module_List._ToString());

                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10701(
                        _Pocket_List, _Module_List,
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID);
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    //MessageBox.Show("10702 Error: " + ex.Message);
                    //throw new Exception("10702 Error: " + ex.Message);

                }
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart13_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            // Wait ACK
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10701", Timestamp_10701,
                    Timer_10701, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))
                {
                    if (ACK == 0)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (timeout)
                    {
                        // T3 Fail
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart14_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v1.0.6.5.3/6.5.4 piezas rechazadas por el MES (70101 y 70106)
            //  Mientras el operador decide en la ventana, este paso no avanza (aunque den START).
            if (esperandoDecisionMes) { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
            //  Eligio no atornillar y mandar a NG: esas piezas se marcan igual que una foto NG y
            //  se vuelve a pedir el 10701 sin ellas (CASE1 = npFlowChart12).
            List<PiezaRechazada> piezasANg = piezasMesANg;
            if (piezasANg != null)
            {
                piezasMesANg = null;
                AplicarNgPiezas(piezasANg);
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            //  Eligio REINTENTAR (otro proceso): lo mismo que Retry en la ventana del MES.
            if (reintentarLoteMes)
            {
                reintentarLoteMes = false;
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK || MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK)
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK)
                    {
                        var objstr = JObject.Parse(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_STR);

                        foreach (var p in objstr["LOTINFOLIST"].Value<JObject>().Properties())
                        {
                            string name = p.Name;
                            JObject jobj = JObject.Parse(p.Value.ToString());

                            string lotid = jobj["LOTID"].Value<string>();
                            string moduleid = jobj["MODULEID"].Value<string>();
                            string procid = jobj["PROCID"].Value<string>();
                            string pordid = jobj["PRODID"].Value<string>();
                            MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID = procid;
                            if (pordid != MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID)
                            {
                                // Alarm
                                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70103"); // report and RCMD not match
                                MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.PauseRunning();
                            }
                        }

                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70106");
                        //MOD@@ Guillermo Carrillo - v1.0.6.5.4 otro proceso (70106)
                        //  La ventana del MES (FrmServerMessage) se quedaba esperando DENTRO de este paso y
                        //  solo decia el texto del MES. Ahora la ventana sale en la pantalla
                        //  (PreguntarLoteFallido) y dice que pieza es; este paso devuelve IDLE hasta que el
                        //  operador decide (ver el principio de este metodo). Reintentar = CASE1, igual que
                        //  el Retry del proveedor; Cancelar = el STOP de siempre.
                        PreguntarLoteFallido();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        ////< A7020 Type = "E" Content = "MES GET LGIT_FAIL" DoStop = "True" />
                        //return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        //JObject failinfo = JObject.Parse(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_STR);
                        //string[] msg = new string[2];
                        //try
                        //{
                        //    msg[0] = failinfo["EQPID"].Value<string>();
                        //    msg[1] = failinfo["TEXT"].Value<string>();
                        //}
                        //catch
                        //{
                        //    msg[0] = "EQPID";
                        //    msg[1] = "LGIT_PP_UPLOAD_FAIL: REASON UNKNOWN";
                        //}
                        ////DisposeFrm(FrmServerMessage);

                        //MESLib.Frms.FrmServerMessage FrmServerMessage = new MESLib.Frms.FrmServerMessage(msg[0], msg[1]);
                        //FrmServerMessage.fnSetTextMessageNShow();
                        //if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                        //{
                        //    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                        //}
                        //else
                        //{
                        //    MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                        //    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        //}
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME.On(60000))
                    {
                        // START OUTTIME
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11002("2");
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("7023");
                        Timer_LOT_START_OUTTIME.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                    return FCResultType.IDLE;
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        DateTime Timestamp_10704 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10704 = new JTimer();
        JTimer Timer_LGIT_LOT_FAIL = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart15_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            PCBCode = new string[12];
            // 107404
            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                JObject tmpobj = JObject.Parse(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_STR);
                //var productcount = JObject.Parse(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_STR)["LOTINFOLIST"].Count();
                //string modelid_from_host = "";
                //bool model_not_match = false;
                // create dictionary id=pocketid, value = data back

                List<string> _PocketID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _ModuleID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> tmpsorted_lotname = new List<string>(); // for sort the information

                foreach (var p in tmpobj["LOTINFOLIST"].Value<JObject>().Properties())
                {
                    string name = p.Name;
                    tmpsorted_lotname.Add(name);
                }
                tmpsorted_lotname.Sort();
                for (int i = 0; i < tmpsorted_lotname.Count; i++)
                {
                    string name = tmpsorted_lotname[i];
                    JObject jobj = tmpobj["LOTINFOLIST"][name].Value<JObject>();

                    int index = -1;
                    // get information from LOT_START
                    string pocketid = jobj["POCKETID"].Value<string>();
                    string module = jobj["MODULEID"].Value<string>();
                    string lotid = jobj["LOTID"].Value<string>();

                    // get index
                    index = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.GetIndex(pocketid,
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs);
                    if (index < 0)
                    {
                        // Alarm
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70104"); // report and RCMD not match
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.PauseRunning();
                    }

                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[index] = lotid;
                    PCBCode[index] = lotid;
                    _PocketID_List.Add(pocketid);
                    _ModuleID_List.Add(module);
                    _LotID_List.Add(lotid);
                }

                Timestamp_10704 = DateTime.Now;
                Timer_10704.Restart();

                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10704");
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Pocket_List: " + _PocketID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Module_List: " + _ModuleID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_LotID_List: " + _LotID_List._ToString());

                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK = false;
                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK = false;
                Timer_LGIT_LOT_FAIL.Restart();

                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10704(_LotID_List,
                    _PocketID_List, _ModuleID_List, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID
                    , MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID);
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart16_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            // 10704 wait ACK
            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10704", Timestamp_10704,
                    Timer_10704, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))

                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");

                    }
                }
                else
                {

                    {
                        // T3 Fail
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart17_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK || MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_OK)
                {
                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_START_OK)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {

                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70106");
                        ////< A7020 Type = "E" Content = "MES GET LGIT_FAIL" DoStop = "True" />
                        //return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        string[] msg = new string[2];
                        try
                        {
                            JObject jobj = JObject.Parse(MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LGIT_LOT_FAIL_STR);
                            msg[0] = jobj["EQPID"].Value<string>();
                            msg[1] = jobj["TEXT"].Value<string>();
                        }
                        catch
                        {
                            msg[0] = "LGIT_LOT_ID_FAIL";
                            msg[1] = "NO TEXT";
                        }
                        //DisposeFrm(FrmServerMessage);

                        MESLib.Frms.FrmServerMessage FrmServerMessage = new MESLib.Frms.FrmServerMessage(msg[0], msg[1]);
                        FrmServerMessage.fnSetTextMessageNShow();
                        if (FrmServerMessage.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                        {
                            return FCResultType.CASE1;
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                            return FCResultType.IDLE;
                        }
                    }
                }
                if (Timer_LGIT_LOT_FAIL.On(1000))
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart20_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                //if (SysPara.screwNGIndex.Count == 0)
                //{
                //    MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.AssembCompeletStatus = true;

                //}
                string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, "SafetyPos");
                motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = Convert.ToInt32(GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut"));
                motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(Convert.ToDouble(pos[2]), Convert.ToDouble(pos[3]), Convert.ToDouble(pos[4]));
                string str = pos[2] + "_" + pos[3] + "_" + pos[4];
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "The axis starts to move to the safe position:GantryFlow", true);

                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.cy_UnderdraughtCylinderUnite.On();


                if (SysPara.EnableMes)
                {
                    if (!MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Contains(true))
                    {
                        if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                        {
                            bFirstEmpty = true;
                            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("First group is empty");
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            bSecondEmpty = true;
                            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("Second group is empty");
                        }
                    }
                    bool bFirst = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");
                    bool bSecond = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSecondGroup");
                    if (bFirst && !bSecond && bFirstEmpty)
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    else if (!bFirst && bSecond && bSecondEmpty)
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    else if (bFirst && bSecond && bFirstEmpty && MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond)//SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    else if (bFirst && bSecond && bSecondEmpty && !MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond)//!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }

                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                return FCResultType.NEXT;


            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        DateTime Timestamp_10710 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10710 = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart21_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10710");
                List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _PocketID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _ModuleID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _CarrierID_List2 = new List<string>();
                List<bool> _Result_List = new List<bool>();
                List<string> _Defect_Code_List = new List<string>();
                List<List<string>> _Material_List = new List<List<string>>();
                List<List<string>> _Material_Type_List = new List<List<string>>();

                List<ScrewTighteningData> screwTightrningDatas = new List<ScrewTighteningData>(SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.ToList());

                for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
                {
                    int index = i;
                    string moduleid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index];
                    foreach (ScrewTighteningData item in screwTightrningDatas)
                    {
                        if (moduleid == item.MatCode)
                        {
                            string pocketid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[index];
                            string module = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index];
                            string lotid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[index];
                            _PocketID_List.Add(pocketid);
                            _ModuleID_List.Add(module);
                            _LotID_List.Add(lotid);
                            _CarrierID_List2.Add(module);
                            bool OK = true;

                            foreach (ScrewTighteningData _item in screwTightrningDatas)
                            {
                                if (_item.MatCode == item.MatCode)
                                {
                                    if (_item.Result != "OK")
                                    {
                                        OK = false;
                                        break;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            _Result_List.Add(OK);

                            if (!OK)
                            {
                                _Defect_Code_List.Add("5"); // have to change to the real defect code, later
                            }
                            else
                            {
                                _Defect_Code_List.Add("");
                            }
                            // get screwid

                            // get bitid
                            string materialid = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ScrewId");
                            string materialtypeid = MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "BitId");

                            List<string> _sub_Material_Id = new List<string>();
                            _sub_Material_Id.Add(materialid);
                            _sub_Material_Id.Add(materialtypeid);

                            List<string> _sub_Material_Type_Id = new List<string>();
                            _sub_Material_Type_Id.Add("12");
                            _sub_Material_Type_Id.Add("13");

                            _Material_List.Add(_sub_Material_Id);
                            _Material_Type_List.Add(_sub_Material_Type_Id);

                            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo(
                                string.Format("module = {0}, lotid = {1}, tray = {2}, result = {3}",
                                 module, lotid, module, item.Result
                                 ));

                            break;
                        }
                    }
                }

                Timestamp_10710 = DateTime.Now;
                Timer_10710.Restart();

                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10710");
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Pocket_List: " + _PocketID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Module_List: " + _ModuleID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_LotID_List: " + _LotID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_CarrierID_List2: " + _CarrierID_List2._ToString());

                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10710(_LotID_List, _PocketID_List, _ModuleID_List,
                    _LotID_List, _PocketID_List, _CarrierID_List2, _ModuleID_List,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID,
                    _Result_List, _Defect_Code_List,
                    _Material_List, _Material_Type_List,
                    "", "");
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart22_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10710", Timestamp_10710,
                    Timer_10710, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))
                {
                    if (ACK == 0)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (timeout)
                    {
                        // T3 Fail
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        DateTime Timestamp_10723 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10723 = new JTimer();
        private FCResultType npFlowChart23_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.EnableMes)
            {
                List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _PocketID_List = new List<string>();
                List<string> _ModuleID_List = new List<string>();

                List<ScrewTighteningData> screwTightrningDatas = new List<ScrewTighteningData>(SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.ToList());
                List<string> _NG_List = new List<string>();
                for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
                {
                    int index = i;
                    string moduleid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index];
                    foreach (ScrewTighteningData item in screwTightrningDatas)
                    {
                        if (moduleid == item.MatCode)
                        {
                            if (item.Result != "OK")
                            {
                                string pocketid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[index];
                                string module = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index];
                                string lotid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[index];


                                if (_NG_List.Contains(module))
                                    continue;
                                _PocketID_List.Add(pocketid);
                                _ModuleID_List.Add(module);
                                _LotID_List.Add(lotid);
                                _NG_List.Add(module);
                                break;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (_LotID_List.Count == 0)
                {
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }

                Timestamp_10723 = DateTime.Now;
                Timer_10401.Restart();
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10723");
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Pocket_List: " + _PocketID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Module_List: " + _ModuleID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_LotID_List: " + _LotID_List._ToString());

                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10723(_LotID_List, _PocketID_List,
                    _ModuleID_List, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID);
            }

            return FCResultType.CASE1;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart25_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                bool LastProduct = false;
                bool bFirst = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");
                bool bSecond = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSecondGroup");

                if (bFirst && !bSecond)
                {
                    LastProduct = true;
                }
                else if (!bFirst && bSecond)
                {
                    LastProduct = true;
                }
                else if (bFirst && bSecond && !MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond)//!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                {
                    LastProduct = true;
                }
                if (LastProduct)
                {
                    Timestamp_10401 = DateTime.Now;
                    Timer_10401.Restart();
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10401(((byte)MESLib.ProcessState.IDLE));
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart26_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

                bool LastProduct = false;

                bool bSecond = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableSecondGroup");

                    LastProduct = true;

                else if (!bFirst && bSecond)

                    LastProduct = true;

                    if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10401", Timestamp_10401,
                        Timer_10401, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))

                button5.BackColor = Color.Green;

            }
        }

        private void btn_GetScrewPos_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            btn_GetScrewPos.Enabled = false;
            SysPara.visionPos = new VisionPos();
            MTR_H1_X.Stop();
            MTR_H1_Y.Stop();
            OB_CamLight.On();
            DelayMs(500);

            CCDPosX = MTR_H1_X.GetEncPos();
            CCDPosY = MTR_H1_Y.GetEncPos();
            try
            {
                double exposure2 = GetSettingValue("MSet", "DisChargeExpTime");
                if (H1_DischargePos.TakePicture(exposure2))                                       //CCD take picture
                {
                    H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["InputImage"].Value = H1_DischargePos.InputImage;
                    string imgSaveName = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss") + "_" + SysPara.screwIndex.ToString() + ".bmp";
                    if (!MiddleLayer.SystemF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "DisableVision"))
                    {

                        H1_DischargePos.RunTB();
                        RefreshDifferentThreadUI(calibRecord, () =>
                        {

                            calibRecord.Image = H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("OutputImage") as CogImage8Grey;
                            calibRecord.Record = H1_DischargePos.TB.CreateLastRunRecord().SubRecords[0];
                            calibRecord.Fit();
                        });



                        bool RunStatus = (bool)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("RunStatus");

                        if ((H1_DischargePos.IsAccept) && (RunStatus))
                        {
                            SysPara.visionPos.x1 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X1");
                            SysPara.visionPos.x2 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X2");
                            SysPara.visionPos.x3 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X3");

                            SysPara.visionPos.y1 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y1");
                            SysPara.visionPos.y2 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y2");
                            SysPara.visionPos.y3 = (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y3");
                            SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = true;
                            //return FCResultType.NEXT;
                        }
                        else if ((H1_DischargePos.IsAccept) && (!RunStatus))
                        {
                            SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                        }
                        else
                        {

                            SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;


                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        OB_CamLight.Off();
                        SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                        //return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    OB_CamLight.Off();
                    SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                    //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1204");
                    //return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                OB_CamLight.Off();
                SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus = false;
                //SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1205");                                                      //CCD fail
                //return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            double tempPosx, tempPosy;
            if (SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus)
            {
                tempPosx = CCDPosX + SysPara.visionPos.x1;
                tempPosy = CCDPosY + SysPara.visionPos.y1;
                Txt_X1.Text = tempPosx.ToString("f3");
                Txt_Y1.Text = tempPosy.ToString("f3");

                tempPosx = CCDPosX + SysPara.visionPos.x2;
                tempPosy = CCDPosY + SysPara.visionPos.y2;

                Txt_X2.Text = tempPosx.ToString("f3");
                Txt_Y2.Text = tempPosy.ToString("f3");

                tempPosx = CCDPosX + SysPara.visionPos.x3;
                tempPosy = CCDPosY + SysPara.visionPos.y3;

                Txt_X3.Text = tempPosx.ToString("f3");
                Txt_Y3.Text = tempPosy.ToString("f3");
            }
            else
            {

                Txt_X1.Text = "0";
                Txt_Y1.Text = "0";

                Txt_X2.Text = "0";
                Txt_Y2.Text = "0";

                Txt_X3.Text = "0";
                Txt_Y3.Text = "0";
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Vision result Error,yes or no?", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {

                }

            }
            OB_CamLight.Off();
            btn_GetScrewPos.Enabled = true;





        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart28_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {




            MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.cy_TransverseCylinderInunite.Off();
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TransverseCylinderOut.Off() && MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_TransverseCylinderIn.On())
            {
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.cy_UnderdraughtCylinderUnite.Off();

                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                CurrentRowsIndex = 18;
                MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.NotifyAssembly = false;

                if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                {
                    scanPosName = 1;
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.bSecondRoundFlag = true;
                    scanPosName = 3;
                }
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = -30;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(5000))
                {

                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1015");
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }


        }

        DateTime Timestamp_10711 = DateTime.Now;
        JTimer Timer_10711 = new JTimer(); private FCResultType npFlowChart31_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            List<ScrewTighteningData> screwTightrningDatas = new List<ScrewTighteningData>(SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.ToList());

            List<string> _LotID_List = new List<string>();
            List<string> _PocketID_List = new List<string>();
            List<string> _ModuleID_List = new List<string>();

            for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODUCEDs.Length; i++)
            {
                int index = i;
                string moduleid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index];
                foreach (ScrewTighteningData item in screwTightrningDatas)
                {
                    if (moduleid == item.MatCode)
                    {
                        string pocketid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[index];
                        string module = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.TRAY_POSITION_QRCODEs[index];
                        string lotid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.LOTIDs[index];
                        _PocketID_List.Add(pocketid);
                        _ModuleID_List.Add(module);
                        _LotID_List.Add(lotid);

                        bool OK = true;

                        foreach (ScrewTighteningData _item in screwTightrningDatas)
                        {
                            if (_item.MatCode == item.MatCode)
                            {
                                int pointstart = -1;
                                switch (index)
                                {
                                    case 0:
                                    case 1:
                                    case 2:
                                    case 9:
                                    case 10:
                                    case 11:
                                        pointstart = index * 3;
                                        break;
                                    case 3:
                                    case 4:
                                    case 5:
                                        pointstart = 6 * 3 + (index - 3) * 3;
                                        break;
                                    case 6:
                                    case 7:
                                    case 8:
                                        pointstart = 3 * 3 + (index - 6) * 3;
                                        break;
                                }

                                // take the last  records of screwdriver
                                if (_item.Point == pointstart + 1)
                                {
                                    Position1Data[index].Time = _item.Time;
                                    Position1Data[index].MatCode = _item.MatCode;
                                    Position1Data[index].Point = _item.Point;
                                    Position1Data[index].Torque = _item.Torque;
                                    Position1Data[index].Angle = _item.Angle;
                                    Position1Data[index].Result = _item.Result;
                                }
                                else if (_item.Point == pointstart + 2)
                                {
                                    Position2Data[index].Time = _item.Time;
                                    Position2Data[index].MatCode = _item.MatCode;
                                    Position2Data[index].Point = _item.Point;
                                    Position2Data[index].Torque = _item.Torque;
                                    Position2Data[index].Angle = _item.Angle;
                                    Position2Data[index].Result = _item.Result;
                                }
                                else if (_item.Point == pointstart + 3)
                                {
                                    Position3Data[index].Time = _item.Time;
                                    Position3Data[index].MatCode = _item.MatCode;
                                    Position3Data[index].Point = _item.Point;
                                    Position3Data[index].Torque = _item.Torque;
                                    Position3Data[index].Angle = _item.Angle;
                                    Position3Data[index].Result = _item.Result;
                                }

                                if (_item.Result != "OK")
                                {
                                    OK = false;
                                    break;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }


            MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10711");

            Dictionary<string, string> parameters = new Dictionary<string, string>();
            string filename = "";
            filename = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmss") + ".csv";
            string filepath = @"D:\GMES\APDLog";
            filepath = System.IO.Path.Combine(filepath, DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd"));

            // check file path
            if (!System.IO.Directory.Exists(filepath))
            {
                System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(filepath);
            }
            string csv_title = "Pallet_BCR,C/T,Vision_Result,Torque_point1,Torque_point2,Torque_point3,Angle1,Angle2,Angle3";
            using (System.IO.StreamWriter sw = new StreamWriter(System.IO.Path.Combine(filepath, filename), true))
            {
                sw.WriteLine(csv_title);
            }

            // create parameters
            string Pallet_BCR = "";
            string ct = "";
            string _ct = (processData.swCycleTime.ElapsedMilliseconds / 1000.0).ToString("#.0");
            string Vision_Result = "";
            string Torque_point1 = "";
            string Torque_point2 = "";
            string Torque_point3 = "";

            string Angle1 = "";//20241025
            string Angle2 = "";//20241025
            string Angle3 = "";//20241025

            for (int i = 0; i < MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs.Length; i++)
            {
                string pocketid = MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs[i];

                if (!_PocketID_List.Contains(pocketid))
                {
                    Pallet_BCR += "";
                    ct += "";
                    Vision_Result += "";
                    Torque_point1 += "";
                    Torque_point2 += "";
                    Torque_point3 += "";
                    Angle1 += "";//20241025
                    Angle2 += "";//20241025
                    Angle3 += "";//20241025
                }
                else
                {
                    int index = _PocketID_List.IndexOf(pocketid);
                    Pallet_BCR += _ModuleID_List[index];
                    ct += _ct;
                    Vision_Result += "OK"; // where is the vision result
                    Torque_point1 += Position1Data[i].Torque.ToString();
                    Torque_point2 += Position2Data[i].Torque.ToString();
                    Torque_point3 += Position3Data[i].Torque.ToString();
                    Angle1 += Position1Data[i].Angle.ToString();//20241025
                    Angle2 += Position2Data[i].Angle.ToString();//20241025
                    Angle3 += Position3Data[i].Angle.ToString();//20241025
                    // csv file parameters                
                    using (System.IO.StreamWriter sw = new StreamWriter(System.IO.Path.Combine(filepath, filename), true))
                    {
                        sw.WriteLine(string.Format("{0},{1},{2},{3},{4},{5},{6},{7},{8}",
                            _ModuleID_List[index],
                            _ct,
                            "OK",
                            Position1Data[i].Torque.ToString(),
                            Position2Data[i].Torque.ToString(),
                            Position3Data[i].Torque.ToString(),
                            Position1Data[i].Angle.ToString(),
                            Position2Data[i].Angle.ToString(),
                            Position3Data[i].Angle.ToString()));
                    }
                }
                if (i != MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.POCKETIDs.Length - 1)
                {
                    Pallet_BCR += ",";
                    ct += ",";
                    Vision_Result += ",";
                    Torque_point1 += ",";
                    Torque_point2 += ",";
                    Torque_point3 += ",";
                    Angle1 += ",";
                    Angle2 += ",";
                    Angle3 += ",";
                }
            }
            parameters.Add("Pallet_BCR", Pallet_BCR);
            parameters.Add("C/T", _ct);
            parameters.Add("Vision_Result", Vision_Result);
            parameters.Add("Torque_point1", Torque_point1);
            parameters.Add("Torque_point2", Torque_point2);
            parameters.Add("Torque_point3", Torque_point3);
            parameters.Add("Angle1", Angle1);
            parameters.Add("Angle2", Angle2);
            parameters.Add("Angle3", Angle3);

            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                Timestamp_10711 = DateTime.Now;
                Timer_10711.Restart();

                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("10711");
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Pocket_List: " + _PocketID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_Module_List: " + _ModuleID_List._ToString());
                MESLib.MesLog.AddInfo("_LotID_List: " + _LotID_List._ToString());


                MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_10711(_LotID_List, _PocketID_List,
                    _ModuleID_List, MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PRODID,
                    MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.PROCID,
                    parameters, filename, filepath, "", "");
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart30_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MESLib.CommParas.EnableMes)
            {
                if (MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_ACK_WithTimeout("10711", Timestamp_10711,
                    Timer_10711, out int ACK, out string data, out bool timeout))
                {
                    if (ACK == 0)
                    {
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70100");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    if (timeout)
                    {
                        // T3 Fail
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesManager.MES_S6F11_11001("2");
                        MESLib.CommParas.MesCenter.StopRunning();
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("70102");
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart32_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                string[] code = new string[6];
                if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond)
                {
                    code[0] = PCBCode[0];
                    code[1] = PCBCode[1];
                    code[2] = PCBCode[2];
                    code[3] = PCBCode[6];
                    code[4] = PCBCode[7];
                    code[5] = PCBCode[8];
                }
                else
                {
                    code[0] = PCBCode[3];
                    code[1] = PCBCode[4];
                    code[2] = PCBCode[5];
                    code[3] = PCBCode[9];
                    code[4] = PCBCode[10];
                    code[5] = PCBCode[11];
                }

                string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\";

                if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath))
                {
                    Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath);
                }

                int imgCount = Math.Min(ProductImages.Count, code.Length);  
                for (int i = 0; i < imgCount; i++)
                {
                    string pcbCode = string.IsNullOrEmpty(code[i]) ? "NA" : code[i];

                    string ImgSaveName = string.Format(@"{0}_{1}_NA_NA_{2}_{3}.bmp",
                        pcbCode,
                        SysPara.TempPalletCode[i],
                        ProductImages[i].Timer,
                        MiddleLayer.MesF2.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EQPID")
                    );

                    string imgPathTem = saveImgPath;  

                    using (Bitmap img = new Bitmap(ProductImages[i].Image))
                    {
                        img.Save(imgPathTem + ImgSaveName, System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat.Bmp);
                    }

                    ProductImages[i].Image.Dispose();
                }

                ProductImages.Clear();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        {

                    npFlowChart33.TaskReset();

                        npFlowChart33.TaskRun();

                t.Start();

            }
            else
            {
                reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Please confirm whether the electric batch cylinder is on the top", true, false, true);
                if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                {

                }
                return;
            }


        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart56_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            OB_SuctionScrewCy.On();
            //Thread.Sleep(1500);
            DelayMs(1200);
            OB_ScrewApp1.On();
            //Thread.Sleep(1500);
            //OB_ScrewVacuum.On();
            //Thread.Sleep(1500);
            DelayMs(1200);
            if (IB_SuctionScrewCy.On())
            {
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else

        private FCResultType npFlowChart58_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //if (!axisAutoFlowTime.On(100))
            //    return FCResultType.IDLE;

            OB_ScrewApp1.On();
            axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;

            if (IsDryCycleMode)
            {
                OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }

            if (IB_ScrewVacuum.On())
            {

                bScrewFeederStartEvent = false;
                ScrewFeederNGNum = 0;
                axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(2000))
                {
                    if (ScrewFeederNGNum > 2)
                    {
                        ScrewFeederNGNum = 0;
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1702");
                    }
                    ScrewFeederNGNum++;
                    //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, "The screw vacuum does not reach the standard value, go to throw the screw", true);
                    bScrewResetFlow = true;
                    bScrewFeederStartEvent = false;
                    return FCResultType.CASE2;
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
