        private FCResultType npFlowChart65_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();

                // IMPORTANT:
                // Torque Retry
                if (mTF6000.Torques > 50)
                {
                    if (getScrewResultNumber < 3)
                    {
                        getScrewResultNumber++;
                        mTF6000.bScrewResult = false;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        mTF6000.bScrewResult = true;
                    }
                }

                // Result Ready
                if (mTF6000.bScrewResult &&mTF6000.bCurveAngle && mTF6000.bCurveTorques)
                {
                    RemainingTimes++;
                    // Time Data
                    List<double> time = new List<double>();
                    double currentTime = 0;
                    // IMPORTANT:
                    // Count一致
                    for (int i = 0; i < mTF6000.CurveTorques.Count; i++)
                    {
                        currentTime += 0.003;
                        time.Add(currentTime);
                    }

                    // Save Result
                    SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.TotalAngle = mTF6000.Angles.ToString();
                    SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.PeekTorque = mTF6000.Torques.ToString();
                    SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.TotalResultString = "OK";
                    screwTimeoutTimer.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }

                // Poll Result
                if (axisAutoFlowTime.On(300))
                {
                    if (!mTF6000.bScrewResult)
                    {
                        mTF6000.GetScrewResult(3);
                        axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }

                    if (!mTF6000.bCurveAngle)
                    {
                        mTF6000.GetScrewResult(1);
                        axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }

                    if (!mTF6000.bCurveTorques)
                    {
                        mTF6000.GetScrewResult(2);
                        axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }

                if (screwTimeoutTimer.On(5000))
                {
                    screwTimeoutTimer.Restart();
                    MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas, "TF6000 Timeout",true);
                    SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1206");
                    return FCResultType.CASE1;
                }
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog( LogType.Alarmas, $"npFlowChart65 Error:{ex}", true);
                SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1206");
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart67_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache == null)
            {
                SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                if ((SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache.Value.TotalAngle == SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.TotalAngle) &&
                    (SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache.Value.PeekTorque == SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.PeekTorque))
                {
                    SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
                else
                {
                    SysPara.screwTightrningDataCache = SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData;
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart66_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            CurrentAngle.Text = SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.TotalAngle;
            CurrentTorque.Text = SysPara.daylilyCheckScrewTightrningData.PeekTorque;
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
            if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 1)
                NowProductQR = SysPara.TempPalletCode.ToArray()[0];
            else if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount > 3)
            {
                float bQR = (SysPara.CacheScrewCount - 1) % 3;
                if (bQR == 0)
                {
                    int index = (SysPara.CacheScrewCount - 1) / 3;
                    if (!MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(!SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                        index = index - 6;
                    NowProductQR = SysPara.TempPalletCode.ToArray()[index];
                }
            }
            ScrewTighteningData screwTighteningData = new ScrewTighteningData();
            screwTighteningData.Time = DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss");
            screwTighteningData.MatCode = NowProductQR;
            screwTighteningData.Point = SysPara.CacheScrewCount;
            if (SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque == "")
            {
                screwTighteningData.Torque = Convert.ToDouble(12.044);
                screwTighteningData.Angle = Convert.ToDouble(1232);
            }
            else
            {
                screwTighteningData.Torque = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque);
                screwTighteningData.Angle = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle);
            }
            //screwTighteningData.Result = SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalStatus ? "OK" : "NG";
            screwTighteningData.Result = SysPara.screwTightrningOKNG ? "OK" : "NG";
            #region
            //if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 1)
            //{
            //    SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree = screwTighteningData.Torque.ToString();
            //    SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree = screwTighteningData.Angle.ToString();
            //}
            //else if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 2)
            //{
            //    SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree += "-"+ screwTighteningData.Torque.ToString();
            //    SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree += "-" + screwTighteningData.Angle.ToString();
            //}
            //else if (SysPara.CacheScrewCount == 3)
            //{
            //    SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree += "-" + screwTighteningData.Torque.ToString();
            //    SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree += "-"+ screwTighteningData.Angle.ToString();

            //    try
            //    {
            //        if (screwTighteningData.Result == "OK")
            //        {
            //            MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(screwTighteningData.MatCode, SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree, SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree);
            //        }
            //        else
            //        {
            //            MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(screwTighteningData.MatCode, "Fail-Fail-Fail", "Fail-Fail-Fail");
            //        }
            //        SysPara.screwTorqueDataForThree = "";
            //        SysPara.screwAngleDataForThree = "";

            //    }
            //    catch (Exception)
            //    {

            //        throw;
            //    }
            //}
            #endregion
            //processData.UpdateResult(screwTighteningData);
            bool bFirst = MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "EnableFirstGroup");
            processData.UpdateResult(screwTighteningData, CurrentRowsIndex, bFirst);
            CurrentRowsIndex++;
            // add one row at the up part, 2023.1.27, zenan
            int maxrows = Convert.ToInt32(MiddleLayer.ProcessF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "MaxDisplay"));
            if (maxrows <= 0)
            {
                maxrows = 12;
            }
            if (processData.fnRowsCount() > maxrows)
            {
                processData.fnRemoveLastResult();
            }

            SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Enqueue(screwTighteningData);

            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Count == 1)
            {
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle = Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle).ToString();
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque = (Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque) * 100000).ToString();
            }
            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Count == 2)
            {
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle += "-" + Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle).ToString();
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque += "-" + (Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque) * 100000).ToString();
            }
            if (SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Count == 3 || screwTighteningData.Result == "NG")
            {
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle += "-" + Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.TotalAngle).ToString();
                SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque += "-" + (Convert.ToDouble(SysPara.screwTightrningData.PeekTorque) * 100000).ToString();
                if (MiddleLayer.ProcessF.bMESEnabled)
                {
                    if (screwTighteningData.Result == "NG")
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(NowProductQR, "Fail-Fail-Fail", "Fail-Fail-Fail");
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        MiddleLayer.MesF.fn_UpdateQR1(NowProductQR, SysPara.ScrewTop1Angle, SysPara.ScrewTop1Torque);
                    }
                    WriteMes(NowProductQR, SysPara.screwTightrningDatas);
                }

                //SysPara.screwTightrningDatas.Clear();
            }
            axisAutoFlowTime.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart68_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)

                }
            }

            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private void button26_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (mTF6000.ConnectStatus)
            {
                //mTF6000.GetScrewResult(2);
                //return;
                mTF6000.bCurveAngle = false;
                mTF6000.bCurveTorques = false;
                mTF6000.GetScrewResult(1);
                //Thread.Sleep(300);
                DelayMs(300);
                mTF6000.GetScrewResult(2);
                //Thread.Sleep(300);
                DelayMs(300);
                mTF6000.GetScrewResult(3);
                //Thread.Sleep(300);
                DelayMs(300);
                List<double> time = new List<double>();
                string path = @"E:\新建文件夹\123.png";
                DateTime timeBase = new DateTime(2020, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0);
                double timeCoefficientOA = timeBase.AddSeconds(0.003).ToOADate() - timeBase.ToOADate();
                double TimeD = 0;

                for (int i = 0; i < 5000; i++)
                {
                    TimeD += timeCoefficientOA;
                    time.Add(TimeD);
                }
                if (mTF6000.bCurveAngle && mTF6000.bCurveTorques)
                {
                    ShowImage(mTF6000.CurveTorques, mTF6000.CurveAngles, time, "0", "123456", mTF6000.Torques, mTF6000.Angles, path);
                }
                else
                {
                    reintento.fnSetTextMessageNShow("Get Curev Data Fill", true, false, true);
                    if (reintento.dResult == DialogResult.Yes)
                    {

                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart75_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            GC.Collect();
            string processName = "FilesMonitor"; // 不包括 .exe
            System.Diagnostics.Process[] processes = System.Diagnostics.Process.GetProcessesByName(processName);

            if (processes.Length > 0)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Program: {processName} existed");
            }
            else
            {
                string exePath = @"D:\CurProgram\FilesMonitor\FilesMonitor.exe";
                string workingDirectory = Path.GetDirectoryName(exePath);

                try
                {
                    System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo startInfo = new System.Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
                    {
                        FileName = exePath,
                        WorkingDirectory = workingDirectory,
                        UseShellExecute = false
                    };

                    System.Diagnostics.Process.Start(startInfo);
                    Console.WriteLine($"Program: {processName} started");
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"Start Program Error: {ex.Message}");
                }
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        #region CI
        #region LOG
        public class CSVLog
        {
            private string csvPathFile;
            private string csvUsername;
            public CSVLog(string path, string username)
            {
                csvPathFile = path;
                csvUsername = username;
                try
                {
                    if (!File.Exists(path))
                        File.Create(path).Close();
                }
                catch
                {
                    //Send alarm that creating file was not possible
                }
            }
            public void AddLog(string info, bool stop = false)
            {
                try
                {
                    StreamWriter sw = File.AppendText(csvPathFile);
                    sw.WriteLine(DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd h:mm:ss tt") + "," + csvUsername + "," + info);
                    sw.Close();
                    if (stop)
                    {
                        SysPara.NPShowAlarm("1523", info);
                        MiddleLayer.StopRun();
                    }
                }
                catch
                {
                    //send alarm that it was not possible to create record
                }
            }
            public enum controlType
            {
                Button = 0,
                RadioButton = 1,
                ComboBox = 2,
                CheckBox = 3,
            }
        }
        public class CSVUsers
        {
            private string csvPathFile;
            public CSVUsers(string path)
            {
                csvPathFile = path;
                try
                {
                    if (!File.Exists(path))
                        File.Create(path).Close();
                }
                catch
                {
                    //Send alarm that creating file was not possible
                }
            } 
            public bool CheckUserIsValid(string username, string password)
            {
                StreamReader sr = new StreamReader(csvPathFile);
                while (!sr.EndOfStream)
                {
                    string[] line = sr.ReadLine().Split(',');
                    if(line[1]==username && line[2] == password)
                        return true;
                }
                return false;
            }
        }
        #endregion

        private void btnLoginCal_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //To enable training interface, the user must initialize the machine.
            operationLog = new CSVLog("D:\\CurProgram\\CSV\\CI_operationLog.csv", txtbUserName.Text);

            if (!bInCalibrationInterface)
            {
                if (SysPara.SystemInitialOk && SysPara.SystemMode == RunMode.IDLE && SysPara.IsMaintenanceMode)
                {
                    operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnLoginCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
                    if (userCredentials.CheckUserIsValid(txtbUserName.Text, txtbPassword.Text))
                    {
                        username = txtbUserName.Text;
                        txtbUserName.ReadOnly = true;
                        txtbPassword.ReadOnly = true;
                        SysPara.SystemInitialOk = false;
                        MiddleLayer.StopRun();
                        loadCalibInterface();
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnLoginCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 1");
                        string sMsgBox = "User credentials are invalid";
                        string sTittle = "ERROR";
                        DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
                    }
                }
                else if (!SysPara.SystemInitialOk)
                {
                    operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnLoginCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 2");
                    string sMsgBox = "You must initialize first";
                    string sTittle = "Initialize";
                    DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
                }
                else if (SysPara.SystemMode == RunMode.AUTO)
                {
                    operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnLoginCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 3");
                    string sMsgBox = "You must stop before operating this interface";
                    string sTittle = "Cannot perform operation";
                    DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
                }
                else if (!SysPara.IsMaintenanceMode)
                {
                    operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnLoginCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 4");
                    string sMsgBox = "You must switch to MANUAL before operating this interface";
                    string sTittle = "Cannot perform operation";
                    DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
                }
            }
            else if (bInCalibrationInterface && !bInPhotoCalibrationProcedure && !bInPhotoMovementProcedure && !bInFasteningProcedure && !bInFasteningMovementProcedure && !bInFasteningCalcMovementProcedure && !bInFasteningSetNewMovementProcedure)
            {
                MiddleLayer.StopRun();
                CCDDataMenu();
                clearGantryData();

                bInCalibrationInterface = false;
                gbManualTesting.Enabled = true;
                gbCalibration.Enabled = false;
                gbGantry.Enabled = false;
                tbcMenu.Enabled = false;
                btnLoginCal.Text = "Login";
                txtbUserName.Text = "";
                txtbPassword.Text = "";
                txtbUserName.ReadOnly = false;
                txtbPassword.ReadOnly = false;
            }
            
        }
        //Position training interface - Aaron 03/19/2024
        //Load data from VPP and data from current positions on the program     
        private void loadCalibInterface()
        {
            bInCalibrationInterface = true;
            btnLoginCal.Text = "LogOut";
            //Load current data
            operationLog = new CSVLog("D:\\CurProgram\\CSV\\CI_operationLog.csv", username);
            alarmLog = new CSVLog("D:\\CurProgram\\CSV\\CI_alarmLog.csv", username);
            processLog = new CSVLog("D:\\CurProgram\\CSV\\CI_processLog.csv", username);

            txtbCCDFasteningPos1.Text = H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechGrab1Pose"].Value.ToString();
            txtbCCDFasteningPos2.Text = H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechGrab2Pose"].Value.ToString();
            txtbCCDFasteningPos3.Text = H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechGrab3Pose"].Value.ToString();
            txtbCCDCameraPos.Text = H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechRobotCCDPose"].Value.ToString();

            gbManualTesting.Enabled = true;
            gbCalibration.Enabled = true;
            gbGantry.Enabled = true;
            gbGantry.Visible = true;
            tbcMenu.Enabled = true;
            foreach (TabPage tp in tbcMenu.TabPages)
            {
                tp.Show();
            }
            tbcMenu.SelectTab(0);
            
        }

                btnTeach.Visible = false;
                btnTeach.Enabled = false;
            }
        }
        //To showcase on the text box, the current coordinates related to the photo position at the selected index - Aaron 03/19/2024
        private void cmbWorkingIndex_SelectedValueChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            operationLog.AddLog("ComboBox: " + cmbWorkingIndex.Name.ToString() + "," + "Working index is changed to: " + cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex.ToString() + "," + "TRUE");

            CCDDataMenu();
            cmbPhotoPos.ComboBox_SetSelectedIndex(cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex);
            cmbFastPosIndex.ComboBox_SetSelectedIndex(cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex);
            
            //Get position from positions data table 
            string photoPosIndex = (cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex + 1).ToString();
            string[] photoPos;
            string photoPosName = "PosName" + photoPosIndex;
            photoPos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, photoPosName);

            txtbPhotoPosX.Text = photoPos[2];
            txtbPhotoPosY.Text = photoPos[3];
            txtbPhotoPosZ.Text = photoPos[4];

            List<string[]> fasteningPos = new List<string[]>();
            string[] fastPos;

            for (int i = 1; i < 4; i++)
            {
                int fasteningPosIndex = (cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex * 3);
                string fasteningPosName = "PosName" + (fasteningPosIndex + i).ToString();
                fastPos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, fasteningPosName);
                fasteningPos.Add(fastPos);
            }

            txtbFasteningPosX1.Text = fasteningPos[0][2];
            txtbFasteningPosY1.Text = fasteningPos[0][3];
            txtbFasteningPosZ1.Text = fasteningPos[0][4];

            txtbFasteningPosX2.Text = fasteningPos[1][2];
            txtbFasteningPosY2.Text = fasteningPos[1][3];
            txtbFasteningPosZ2.Text = fasteningPos[1][4];

            txtbFasteningPosX3.Text = fasteningPos[2][2];
            txtbFasteningPosY3.Text = fasteningPos[2][3];
            txtbFasteningPosZ3.Text = fasteningPos[2][4];

            txtbNewFastPosX1.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosY1.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosZ1.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosX2.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosY2.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosZ2.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosX3.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosY3.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosZ3.Text = "";

            fasteningPos.Clear();
        }

        private void btnTeach_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex == -1)
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnTeach.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 1");

                lblGoToPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Red);
            }
            else
            {
                CCDDataMenu();
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnTeach.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
                lblGoToPhotoResult.Text = "CALIBRATING";
                lblGoToPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Black);
                photoPosCalInterrface = cmbPhotoPos.SelectedIndex + 1;
                tbcMenu.SelectTab(1);
                btnStartPhotoCalib.Enabled = true;
            }
        }
        private void btnSaveNewFastPos_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (cmbWorkingIndex.SelectedIndex == -1)
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnSaveNewFastPos.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 1");
                lblGoToPhotoResult.Text = "ERROR";
                lblGoToPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Red);
            }
            else
            {
                CCDDataMenu();
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnSaveNewFastPos.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
                lblGoToPhotoResult.Text = "NA";
                lblGoToPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Black);
                if(!updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 1), txtbNewFastPosX1.Text, txtbNewFastPosY1.Text))
                {
                    updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 1), txtbFasteningPosX1.Text, txtbFasteningPosY1.Text);
                }
                if(!updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 2), txtbNewFastPosX2.Text, txtbNewFastPosY2.Text))
                {
                    updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 1), txtbFasteningPosX2.Text, txtbFasteningPosY2.Text);
                }
                if(!updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 3), txtbNewFastPosX3.Text, txtbNewFastPosY3.Text))
                {
                    updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 1), txtbFasteningPosX3.Text, txtbFasteningPosY3.Text);
                }
                loadCalibInterface();
                clearGantryData();
            }
        }

        #region Move to photo pos PROCESS FLOW 

        private void btnStartMoveToPhoto_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.IsMaintenanceMode)
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStartMoveToPhoto.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 1");
                string sMsgBox = "Switch to AUTO";
                string sTittle = "Not allowed to perform in Manual Mode";
                DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
            }
            else
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStartMoveToPhoto.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
                bInPhotoMovementProcedure = false;
                Thread.Sleep(250);
                btnStartMoveToPhoto.Enabled = false;
                bInPhotoMovementProcedure = true;
                Task t = new Task(() =>
                {
                    npFlowChart2001.TaskReset();
                    while (bInPhotoMovementProcedure)
                    {
                        npFlowChart2001.TaskRun();
                        Thread.Sleep(5);
                    }
                });
                t.Start();
            }
        }
        private void btnStopMoveToPhoto_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStopMoveToPhoto.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            bInPhotoMovementProcedure = false;
            tbcMenu.SelectTab(0);
            MiddleLayer.StopRun();
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart2001_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2001.Name + "," + npFlowChart2001.Text + "," + "TRUE");
            cy_ScrewLockingCylinderunite.On();
            OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
            moveToPhotoTimer.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart2002_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (in_PalletInPlace.On(200) && in_JackinCylinderUp.On(200) && in_PressingFixtureDown.On(200) && in_ScrewBitUpPos.On(200) && in_ScrewBitFasteningPos.Off() && in_ScrewBitFeedPos.Off())
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2002.Name + "," + npFlowChart2002.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                moveToPhotoTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToPhotoTimer.On(2000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2002.Name + "," + npFlowChart2002.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2002.Name + "," + npFlowChart2002.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToPhotoTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart2003_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string photoPosIndex = (photoPosCalInterrface).ToString();

            string[] pos;
            string photoPosName = "PosName" + photoPosIndex;
            pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, photoPosName);
            posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
            posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
            posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);

            //MTR_H1_X.WorkSpeed = 200;
            //MTR_H1_Y.WorkSpeed = 200;
            //MTR_H1_Z.WorkSpeed = 200;
            AxisParaSetting();
            MTR_H1_X.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Y.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Z.WorkSpeed = 50;

            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = false;
            motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = posZ;
            motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = Convert.ToInt32(GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut"));
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
            moveToPhotoTimer.Restart();
            processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2003.Name + "," + npFlowChart2003.Text + ": " + posX.ToString() + posY.ToString() + posZ.ToString() + "," + "TRUE");
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart2004_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2004.Name + "," + npFlowChart2004.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                moveToPhotoTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToPhotoTimer.On(10000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2004.Name + "," + npFlowChart2004.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2004.Name + "," + npFlowChart2004.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToPhotoTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart2005_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            processLog.AddLog("Move to photo process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart2005.Name + "," + npFlowChart2005.Text + "," + "TRUE");

            btnTriggerPhoto.Enabled = true;
            tbcMenu.SelectTab(0);
            locatedAtPhotoPos = photoPosCalInterrface;
            highLightPhotoPos(photoPosCalInterrface);
            photoPosCalInterrface = -1;
            MiddleLayer.StopRun();
            bInPhotoMovementProcedure = false;
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        #endregion

        private void btnTriggerPhoto_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            //Add check if in pos, only trigger if in pos
            operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnTriggerPhoto.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
            CCDDataMenu(false);
            OB_CamLight.On();
            triggerManualPhoto();
            OB_CamLight.Off();
            calculateFasteningPos();
            checkIfInRange();
        }
        private void triggerManualPhoto()
        {
            double currentCCDPosX = MTR_H1_X.GetEncPos();
            double currentCCDPosY = MTR_H1_Y.GetEncPos();
            try
            {
                double exposure2 = GetSettingValue("MSet", "DisChargeExpTime");
                if (H1_DischargePos.TakePicture(exposure2))                                       //CCD take picture
                {
                    H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["InputImage"].Value = H1_DischargePos.InputImage;
                    H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["OrderIndex"].Value = SysPara.OrderIndex;

                    H1_DischargePos.RunTB();
                    RefreshDifferentThreadUI(cogDisplayCalib, () =>
                    {
                        cogDisplayCalib.Image = H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("OutputImage") as CogImage8Grey;
                        cogDisplayCalib.Record = H1_DischargePos.TB.CreateLastRunRecord().SubRecords[0];
                        cogDisplayCalib.Fit();
                    });

                    bool RunStatus = (bool)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("RunStatus");

                    if ((H1_DischargePos.IsAccept) && (RunStatus))
                    {
                        lblTriggerPhotoResult.Text = "SUCCESFUL";
                        lblTriggerPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Black);

                        calcScrewPos.x1 = currentCCDPosX + (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X1");
                        calcScrewPos.x2 = currentCCDPosX + (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X2");
                        calcScrewPos.x3 = currentCCDPosX + (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("X3");

                        calcScrewPos.y1 = currentCCDPosY + (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y1");
                        calcScrewPos.y2 = currentCCDPosY + (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y2");
                        calcScrewPos.y3 = currentCCDPosY + (double)H1_DischargePos.GetOutput("Y3");
                    }
                    else if ((H1_DischargePos.IsAccept) && (!RunStatus))
                    {
                        lblTriggerPhotoResult.Text = "ERROR";
                        lblTriggerPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Red);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        lblTriggerPhotoResult.Text = "FAILURE";
                        lblTriggerPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Red);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    OB_CamLight.Off();
                    lblTriggerPhotoResult.Text = "FAILURE";
                    lblTriggerPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Red);
                }
            }
            catch
            {
                OB_CamLight.Off();
                lblTriggerPhotoResult.Text = "FAILURE";                                                   //CCD fail
                lblTriggerPhotoResult.SetForeColor(Color.Red);

            }
        }
        private void saveManualPhoto()
        {
            bool isSave = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "ImgSave");
            bool isJPGSave = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "JPEGImgSave");
            string imgSaveName = DateTime.Now.ToString("HH_mm_ss") + "_" + SysPara.screwIndex.ToString() + ".bmp";

            if (isSave)
            {
                string saveImgPath = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("PSet", "SaveImagePath") + "\\" + "BMP\\" + DateTime.Now.ToString("yyyyMMdd") + "\\";
                using (CogImageFileBMP objImg = new CogImageFileBMP())
                {
                    if (!Directory.Exists(saveImgPath)) { Directory.CreateDirectory(saveImgPath); }
                    objImg.Open(saveImgPath + imgSaveName, CogImageFileModeConstants.Write);
                    objImg.Append(H1_DischargePos.InputImage);
                    objImg.Close();
                }
            }

        }

        {

                    btnPos4.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;

                    break;
                case 6:
                    btnPos6.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                case 7:
                    btnPos7.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                case 8:
                    btnPos8.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                case 9:
                    btnPos9.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                case 10:
                    btnPos10.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                case 11:
                    btnPos11.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                case 12:
                    btnPos12.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.LawnGreen);
                    break;
                default:
                    btnPos1.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.Transparent);
                    btnPos2.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.Transparent);

                    btnPos12.SetBackColor(System.Drawing.Color.Transparent);

        }
        private void calculateFasteningPos()
        {
            //if photo fails, disable and reset CCD panel controls
            if (locatedAtPhotoPos > 0 && locatedAtPhotoPos < 13)
            {
                txtbCalFastPosX1.Text = calcScrewPos.x1.ToString();
                txtbCalFastPosX2.Text = calcScrewPos.x2.ToString();
                txtbCalFastPosX3.Text = calcScrewPos.x3.ToString();
                txtbCalFastPosY1.Text = calcScrewPos.y1.ToString();
                txtbCalFastPosY2.Text = calcScrewPos.y2.ToString();
                txtbCalFastPosY3.Text = calcScrewPos.y3.ToString();
            }
        }
        private void checkIfInRange()
        {
            string[] pos_Screw1 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, "PosName" + (((locatedAtPhotoPos-1) * 3) + 1).ToString());
            string[] pos_Screw2 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, "PosName" + (((locatedAtPhotoPos-1) * 3) + 2).ToString());
            string[] pos_Screw3 = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, "PosName" + (((locatedAtPhotoPos-1) * 3) + 3).ToString());
            bool[] inRange = new bool[6];
            inRange[0] = Math.Abs(calcScrewPos.x1 - double.Parse(pos_Screw1[2])) < 2;
            inRange[1] = Math.Abs(calcScrewPos.y1 - double.Parse(pos_Screw1[3])) < 2;
            inRange[2] = Math.Abs(calcScrewPos.x2 - double.Parse(pos_Screw2[2])) < 2;
            inRange[3] = Math.Abs(calcScrewPos.y2 - double.Parse(pos_Screw2[3])) < 2;
            inRange[4] = Math.Abs(calcScrewPos.x3 - double.Parse(pos_Screw3[2])) < 2;
            inRange[5] = Math.Abs(calcScrewPos.y3 - double.Parse(pos_Screw3[3])) < 2;

            if (inRange[0] && inRange[1])
            {
                lblPos1InRange.Text = "IN RANGE";
                btnGoToCalFastPos1.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                lblPos1InRange.Text = "OUT OF RANGE";
                btnGoToCalFastPos1.Enabled = false;
            }

            if (inRange[2] && inRange[3])
            {
                lblPos2InRange.Text = "IN RANGE";
                btnGoToCalFastPos2.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                lblPos2InRange.Text = "OUT OF RANGE";
                btnGoToCalFastPos2.Enabled = false;
            }

            if (inRange[4] && inRange[5])
            {
                lblPos3InRange.Text = "IN RANGE";
                btnGoToCalFastPos3.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                lblPos3InRange.Text = "OUT OF RANGE";
                btnGoToCalFastPos2.Enabled = false;
            }
        }
        private bool[] checkIfFasteningPosInRange(int index, string xVal, string yVal, string zVal, int acceptableRange)
        {
            //Add safety for index overLoad
            bool[] ValInRange = new bool[3];
            string[] pos_Screw = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, "PosName" + index.ToString());
            if (!(xVal == null) && xVal!= "")
            {
                ValInRange[0] = Math.Abs(double.Parse(xVal) - double.Parse(pos_Screw[2])) < acceptableRange;
            }
            else
            {
                ValInRange[0] = false;
            }
            if (!(yVal == null) && yVal != "")
            {
                ValInRange[1] = Math.Abs(double.Parse(yVal) - double.Parse(pos_Screw[3])) < acceptableRange;
            }
            else
            {
                ValInRange[1] = false;
            }
            if (!(zVal == null) && zVal != "")
            {
                ValInRange[2] = Math.Abs(double.Parse(zVal) - double.Parse(pos_Screw[4])) < acceptableRange;
            }
            {
                ValInRange[2] = false;
            }
                return ValInRange;
        }
        private bool checkIfCurrentPos()
        {
            return false;
        }
        private void CCDDataMenu(bool disabletriggerButton = true, bool clearData = true, bool resetCCDButtonInteraction = true, bool clearCCDRecords = true)
        {
            btnTriggerPhoto.Enabled = !disabletriggerButton;

            if (clearData)
            {
                txtbCurrentPhotoPos.Text = "";
                txtbCalFastPosX1.Text = "";
                txtbCalFastPosX2.Text = "";
                txtbCalFastPosX3.Text = "";
                txtbCalFastPosY1.Text = "";
                txtbCalFastPosY2.Text = "";
                txtbCalFastPosY3.Text = "";
                lblPos1InRange.Text = "NA";
                lblPos2InRange.Text = "NA";
                lblPos3InRange.Text = "NA";
            }

            if (resetCCDButtonInteraction)
            {
                btnGoToCalFastPos1.Enabled = false;
                btnGoToCalFastPos2.Enabled = false;
                btnGoToCalFastPos3.Enabled = false;
                enableFasteningIndex = 0;
                calculatedIndex = 0;
                btnFastenAllPos.Enabled = false;
                btnFastenPos1.Enabled = false;
                btnFastenPos2.Enabled = false;
                btnFastenPos3.Enabled = false;
                btnFastenPos1.Visible = false;
                btnFastenPos2.Visible = false;
                btnFastenPos3.Visible = false;
                highLightPhotoPos(0);
            }

            if (clearCCDRecords)
            {
                cogDisplayCalib.ClearImage8GreyColorMap();
            }
            

        }
        private void clearGantryData()
        {
            locatedAtPhotoPos = -1;
            photoPosCalInterrface = -1;
            fasteningPosCalInterface = -1;
            bSetNewFinished = false;
            setNewIndex = 0;
            calculatedIndex = 0;
            enableFasteningIndex = 0;
            stepSequence = 0;
            fasteningStepTrigger = false;

            txtbPhotoPosX.Text = "";
            txtbPhotoPosY.Text = "";
            txtbPhotoPosZ.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosX1.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosY1.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosZ1.Text = "";

            txtbFasteningPosX2.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosY2.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosZ2.Text = "";

            txtbFasteningPosX3.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosY3.Text = "";
            txtbFasteningPosZ3.Text = "";

            txtbNewFastPosX1.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosY1.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosZ1.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosX2.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosY2.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosZ2.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosX3.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosY3.Text = "";
            txtbNewFastPosZ3.Text = "";
        }

        private bool updateFasteningPosTable(int index, string xVal, string yVal, bool updateZ = false, string zVal = null)
        {
            string fasteningPosName = "PosName" + index.ToString();
            string[] pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, fasteningPosName);
            bool[] inRange = checkIfFasteningPosInRange(index, xVal, yVal, zVal, 4);
            if (!updateZ && xVal != null && yVal != null && inRange[0] && inRange[1])
            {
                string[] updateFastPos1 = new string[] { xVal, yVal, pos[4]};
                dgv_AssemblyScrewPos.UpdateGridViewRow((int.Parse(pos[0])-1), 2, 4, updateFastPos1);
                return true;
            }
            else if(updateZ && xVal != null && yVal != null && zVal != null && inRange[0] && inRange[1] && inRange[2])
            {
                string[] updateFastPos1 = new string[] { xVal, yVal, zVal };
                dgv_AssemblyScrewPos.UpdateGridViewRow((int.Parse(pos[0])-1), 2, 4, updateFastPos1);
                return true;
            }
            return false; 
        }


        #region Move to set new fastening pos PROCESS FLOW 
        private void btnStartScrewBitCal_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.IsMaintenanceMode)
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStartScrewBitCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 1");
                string sMsgBox = "Switch to AUTO";
                string sTittle = "Not allowed to perform in Manual Mode";
                DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
            }
            else
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStartScrewBitCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
                bInFasteningSetNewMovementProcedure = false;
                Thread.Sleep(250);
                btnStartScrewBitCal.Enabled = false;
                bInFasteningSetNewMovementProcedure = true;
                Task t = new Task(() =>
                {
                    npFlowChart1001.TaskReset();
                    while (bInFasteningSetNewMovementProcedure)
                    {
                        npFlowChart1001.TaskRun();
                        Thread.Sleep(5);
                    }
                });
                t.Start();
            }   
        }
        private void btnStopScrewBitCal_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStopScrewBitCal.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");

            btnJogZN.Enabled = false;
            btnJogZP.Enabled = false;
            btnJogYN.Enabled = false;
            btnJogYP.Enabled = false;
            btnJogXN.Enabled = false;
            btnJogXP.Enabled = false;

            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            bInFasteningSetNewMovementProcedure = false;
            tbcMenu.SelectTab(0);
            MiddleLayer.StopRun();
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart1002_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (in_PalletInPlace.On(200) && in_JackinCylinderUp.On(200) && in_PressingFixtureDown.On(200) && in_ScrewBitUpPos.On(200) && in_ScrewBitFasteningPos.Off() && in_ScrewBitFeedPos.Off())
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1002.Name + "," + npFlowChart1002.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                moveToFasteningSetNewPosTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToFasteningSetNewPosTimer.On(2000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1002.Name + "," + npFlowChart1002.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1002.Name + "," + npFlowChart1002.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToFasteningSetNewPosTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart1003_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string fasteningPosIndex = fasteningPosCalInterface.ToString();

            string[] pos;
            string fasteningPosName = "PosName" + fasteningPosIndex;
            pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, fasteningPosName);
            posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
            posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
            posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]) - 10;

            
            AxisParaSetting();
            MTR_H1_X.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Y.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Z.WorkSpeed = 50;

            //MTR_H1_X.SpeedRatio = 20;
            //MTR_H1_Y.SpeedRatio = 20;
            //MTR_H1_Z.SpeedRatio = 20;

            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
            motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = 0;
            motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = Convert.ToInt32(GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut"));
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
            moveToFasteningSetNewPosTimer.Restart();
            processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1003.Name + "," + npFlowChart1003.Text + ": " + posX.ToString() + posY.ToString() + posZ.ToString() + "," + "TRUE");
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart1006_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            int showLogAttempts = 0;
            if (!SysPara.IsMaintenanceMode)
            {
                btnUnlock.Enabled = false;
                btnJogZN.Enabled = false;
                btnJogZP.Enabled = false;
                btnJogYN.Enabled = false;
                btnJogYP.Enabled = false;
                btnJogXN.Enabled = false;
                btnJogXP.Enabled = false;
                btnCylinderUp.Enabled = false;
                btnCylinderDown.Enabled = false;
            }
            else
            {
                btnUnlock.Enabled = true;
                btnCylinderUp.Enabled = true;
                btnCylinderDown.Enabled = true;
            }

            if (bSetNewFinished == true)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1006.Name + "," + npFlowChart1006.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                showLogAttempts = 0;
                btnJogZN.Enabled = false;
                btnJogZP.Enabled = false;
                btnJogYN.Enabled = false;
                btnJogYP.Enabled = false;
                btnJogXN.Enabled = false;
                btnJogXP.Enabled = false;
                bInFasteningSetNewJoggingProcedure = false;
                cy_ScrewLockingCylinderunite.On();
                OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
                moveToFasteningSetNewPosTimer.Restart();
                bSetNewFinished = false;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if(showLogAttempts == 0)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1006.Name + "," + npFlowChart1006.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                showLogAttempts = 1;
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

                    break;
                case 2:
                    txtbNewFastPosX2.Text = MTR_H1_X.GetEncPos().ToString();
                    txtbNewFastPosY2.Text = MTR_H1_Y.GetEncPos().ToString();
                    txtbNewFastPosZ2.Text = MTR_H1_Z.GetEncPos().ToString();
                    processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1008.Name + "," + npFlowChart1008.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                    break;
                case 3:
                    txtbNewFastPosX3.Text = MTR_H1_X.GetEncPos().ToString();
                    txtbNewFastPosY3.Text = MTR_H1_Y.GetEncPos().ToString();
                    txtbNewFastPosZ3.Text = MTR_H1_Z.GetEncPos().ToString();
                    processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1008.Name + "," + npFlowChart1008.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                    break;
                default:
                    processLog.AddLog("Set new position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart1008.Name + "," + npFlowChart1008.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                    break;
            }
            tbcMenu.SelectTab(0);
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }

        private void btnStartPhotoCalib_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (SysPara.IsMaintenanceMode)
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStartPhotoCalib.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "FALSE 1");
                string sMsgBox = "Switch to AUTO";
                string sTittle = "Not allowed to perform in Manual Mode";
                DialogResult result = MessageBox.Show(new Form { TopMost = true }, sMsgBox, sTittle, MessageBoxButtons.OKCancel, MessageBoxIcon.Question, MessageBoxDefaultButton.Button1, (MessageBoxOptions)0x40000);
            }
            else
            {
                operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStartPhotoCalib.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");
                bInPhotoCalibrationProcedure = false;
                Thread.Sleep(250);
                btnStartPhotoCalib.Enabled = false;
                bInPhotoCalibrationProcedure = true;
                Task t = new Task(() =>
                {
                    npFlowChart0001.TaskReset();
                    while (bInPhotoCalibrationProcedure)
                    {
                        npFlowChart0001.TaskRun();
                        Thread.Sleep(5);
                    }
                });
                t.Start();
            }
        }
        private void btnStopPhotoCalib_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            operationLog.AddLog("Button: " + btnStopPhotoCalib.Name.ToString() + "," + "Is clicked" + "," + "TRUE");

            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            bInPhotoCalibrationProcedure = false;
            tbcMenu.SelectTab(0);
            MiddleLayer.StopRun();
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart0001_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0001.Name + "," + npFlowChart0001.Text + "," + "TRUE");
            cy_ScrewLockingCylinderunite.On();
            OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
            moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.Restart();
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart0002_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (in_PalletInPlace.On(200) && in_JackinCylinderUp.On(200) && in_PressingFixtureDown.On(200) && in_ScrewBitUpPos.On(200) && in_ScrewBitFasteningPos.Off() && in_ScrewBitFeedPos.Off())
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0002.Name + "," + npFlowChart0002.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.On(2000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0002.Name + "," + npFlowChart0002.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0002.Name + "," + npFlowChart0002.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart0003_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            
            string photoPosIndex = photoPosCalInterrface.ToString();

            string[] pos;
            string photoPosName = "PosName" + photoPosIndex;
            pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, photoPosName);
            posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
            posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
            posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);

            AxisParaSetting();
            MTR_H1_X.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Y.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Z.WorkSpeed = 50;
            //MTR_H1_X.SpeedRatio = 50;
            //MTR_H1_Y.SpeedRatio = 50;
            //MTR_H1_Z.SpeedRatio = 50;

            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = false;
            motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = posZ;
            motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = Convert.ToInt32(GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut"));
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
            moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.Restart();
            processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0003.Name + "," + npFlowChart0003.Text + ": " + posX.ToString() + posY.ToString() + posZ.ToString() + "," + "TRUE");
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart0004_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        { 
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0004.Name + "," + npFlowChart0004.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.On(10000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0004.Name + "," + npFlowChart0004.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0004.Name + "," + npFlowChart0004.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart0006_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0006.Name + "," + npFlowChart0006.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechGrab1Pose"].Value = txtbNewFastPosX1.Text + "," + txtbNewFastPosY1.Text + "," + txtbNewFastPosZ1.Text;
                //H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechGrab2Pose"].Value = txtbNewFastPosX2.Text + "," + txtbNewFastPosY2.Text + "," + txtbNewFastPosZ2.Text;
                //H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechGrab3Pose"].Value = txtbNewFastPosX3.Text + "," + txtbNewFastPosY3.Text + "," + txtbNewFastPosZ3.Text;
                H1_DischargePos.TB.Inputs["TechCCDPose"].Value = H1_DischargePos.TB.Outputs["CurCCDPose"].Value;
                updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 1), txtbNewFastPosX1.Text, txtbNewFastPosY1.Text);
                //updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 2), txtbNewFastPosX2.Text, txtbNewFastPosY2.Text);
                //updateFasteningPosTable((cmbFastPosIndex.SelectedIndex * 3 + 3), txtbNewFastPosX3.Text, txtbNewFastPosY3.Text);

                moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            catch
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0006.Name + "," + npFlowChart0006.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Bit/CCD calibration process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart0006.Name + "," + npFlowChart0006.Text + " failed: " + moveToCalibratePhotoTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart3002_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (in_PalletInPlace.On(200) && in_JackinCylinderUp.On(200) && in_PressingFixtureDown.On(200) && in_ScrewBitUpPos.On(200) && in_ScrewBitFasteningPos.Off() && in_ScrewBitFeedPos.Off())
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3002.Name + "," + npFlowChart3002.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.On(2000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3002.Name + "," + npFlowChart3002.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3002.Name + "," + npFlowChart3002.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);

        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart3003_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            
            string fasteningPosIndex = fasteningPosCalInterface.ToString();

            string[] pos;
            string fasteningPosName = "PosName" + fasteningPosIndex;
            pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_AssemblyScrewPos, fasteningPosName);
            posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
            posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
            posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);

            AxisParaSetting();
            MTR_H1_X.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Y.WorkSpeed = 50;
            MTR_H1_Z.WorkSpeed = 50;

            switch (calculatedIndex)
            {
                case 0:
                    posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                    posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                    posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                    break;
                case 1:
                    posX = calcScrewPos.x1;
                    posY = calcScrewPos.y1;
                    posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                    break;
                case 2:
                    posX = calcScrewPos.x2;
                    posY = calcScrewPos.y2;
                    posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                    break;
                case 3:
                    posX = calcScrewPos.x3;
                    posY = calcScrewPos.y3;
                    posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);
                    break;
                default:
                    //return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    break;
            }

            motorMoveCtrl.Stop();
            motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = true;
            motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = posZ;
            motorMoveCtrl.MoveTimeOut = Convert.ToInt32(GetSettingValue("MSet", "MoveTimeOut"));
            motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
            moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.Restart();
            processLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3003.Name + "," + npFlowChart3003.Text + ": " + posX.ToString() + posY.ToString() + posZ.ToString() + "," + "TRUE");

            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart3004_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (motorMoveCtrl.bMoveComplete && motorMoveCtrl.bExeComplete)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3004.Name + "," + npFlowChart3004.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                switch (calculatedIndex)
                {
                    case 0:
                        enableFasteningIndex = 0;
                        //Add BUTTON to enable manual fastening
                        break;
                    case 1:
                        enableFasteningIndex = 1;
                        btnGoToCalFastPos1.Enabled = false;
                        btnFastenPos1.Enabled = true;
                        btnFastenPos1.Visible = true;
                        break;
                    case 2:
                        enableFasteningIndex = 2;
                        btnGoToCalFastPos2.Enabled = false;
                        btnFastenPos2.Enabled = true;
                        btnFastenPos2.Visible = true;
                        break;
                    case 3:
                        enableFasteningIndex = 3;
                        btnGoToCalFastPos3.Enabled = false;
                        btnFastenPos3.Enabled = true;
                        btnFastenPos3.Visible = true;
                        break;
                    default:
                        break;
                }
                moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.Restart();
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else if (moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.On(10000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3004.Name + "," + npFlowChart3004.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3004.Name + "," + npFlowChart3004.Text + " timeOut: " + moveToFasteningCalcPosTimer.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart3005_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            processLog.AddLog("Move to calculated fastening position process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart3005.Name + "," + npFlowChart3005.Text + "," + "TRUE");

        private FCResultType npFlowChart4001_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4001.Name + "," + npFlowChart4001.Text + "," + "TRUE");
            fasteningProcess.Restart();
            if (chkbEnableSbyS.Checked)
            {
                chkbEnableSbyS.Enabled = false;
                btnNextStep.Visible = true;
                btnNextStep.Enabled = true;
            }
            else
            {
                btnNextStep.Visible = false;
                btnNextStep.Enabled = false;
            }
            return FCResultType.NEXT;
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart4002_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (in_PalletInPlace.On(200) && in_JackinCylinderUp.On(200) && in_PressingFixtureDown.On(200) && in_ScrewBitUpPos.On(200) && in_ScrewBitFasteningPos.Off() && in_ScrewBitFeedPos.Off())
            {
                if (chkbEnableSbyS.Checked)
                {
                    if (fasteningStepTrigger)
                    {
                        processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4002.Name + "," + npFlowChart4002.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                        fasteningStepTrigger = false;
                        fasteningProcess.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4002.Name + "," + npFlowChart4002.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                    fasteningProcess.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else if (fasteningProcess.On(2000))
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4002.Name + "," + npFlowChart4002.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                alarmLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4002.Name + "," + npFlowChart4002.Text + " timeOut: " + fasteningProcess.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else
            {
                cy_ScrewLockingCylinderunite.On();
                OB_SuctionScrewCy.Off();
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }
        private FCResultType npFlowChart4003_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IB_ScrewRing.On())
            {
                stepSequence = 1;
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else if(stepSequence == 1)
            {
                OB_ScrewFeederStart.Off();
                if (chkbEnableSbyS.Checked)
                {
                    if (fasteningStepTrigger)
                    {
                        processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4003.Name + "," + npFlowChart4003.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                        stepSequence = 0;
                        fasteningStepTrigger = false;
                        fasteningProcess.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4003.Name + "," + npFlowChart4003.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                    stepSequence = 0;
                    fasteningProcess.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else if (fasteningProcess.On(2000) && stepSequence != 1)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4003.Name + "," + npFlowChart4003.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                stepSequence = 0;
                alarmLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4003.Name + "," + npFlowChart4003.Text + " timeOut: " + fasteningProcess.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else
            {
                OB_ScrewFeederStart.On();
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
        }

        private FCResultType npFlowChart4007_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (IB_ScrewDriverReady.On() && IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.Off() && stepSequence == 0)
            {
                OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                DelayMs(100);
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.On();
                stepSequence = 1;
            }
            else if (IB_ScrewDriverReady.Off() && IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.Off() && stepSequence==1)
            {
                OB_ScrewDriverStart.Off();
                stepSequence = 2;
            }
            else if (IB_ScrewDriverReady.On() && IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.Off() && IB_ScrewFasteningOK.On() && stepSequence == 2)
            {
                stepSequence = 3;
            }
            else if (IB_ScrewDriverReady.On() && IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.Off() && IB_ScrewFasteningNG.On() && stepSequence == 2)
            {
                stepSequence = 3;
            }
            else if (IB_ScrewDriverReady.On() && IB_ScrewFeederAlarm.On() && stepSequence == 2)
            {
                stepSequence = 0;
            }
            else if(stepSequence == 3)
            {
                if (chkbEnableSbyS.Checked)
                {
                    if (fasteningStepTrigger)
                    {
                        processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4007.Name + "," + npFlowChart4007.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                        stepSequence = 0;
                        fasteningStepTrigger = false;
                        fasteningProcess.Restart();
                        return FCResultType.NEXT;
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        return FCResultType.IDLE;
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4007.Name + "," + npFlowChart4007.Text + "," + "TRUE");
                    stepSequence = 0;
                    fasteningProcess.Restart();
                    return FCResultType.NEXT;
                }
            }
            else if (fasteningProcess.On(2500) && stepSequence != 3)
            {
                processLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4007.Name + "," + npFlowChart4007.Text + "," + "FALSE 1");
                stepSequence = 0;
                alarmLog.AddLog("Fastening process" + "," + "Flow Chart: " + npFlowChart4007.Name + "," + npFlowChart4007.Text + " timeOut: " + fasteningProcess.GetValue().ToString() + "s" + "," + "STOP",true);
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            else
            {
                OB_ScrewApp1.On();
                return FCResultType.IDLE;
            }
            return FCResultType.IDLE;
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart100_FlowRun(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            double tempPosx, tempPosy;
            if (SysPara.CCDAlogrithmStatus)
            {
                tempPosx = CCDPosX + SysPara.visionPos.x1;
                tempPosy = CCDPosY + SysPara.visionPos.y1;
                cacheTightenPos.Add(new Cog3DVect2(tempPosx, tempPosy));
                tempPosx = CCDPosX + SysPara.visionPos.x2;
                tempPosy = CCDPosY + SysPara.visionPos.y2;
                cacheTightenPos.Add(new Cog3DVect2(tempPosx, tempPosy));
                tempPosx = CCDPosX + SysPara.visionPos.x3;
                tempPosy = CCDPosY + SysPara.visionPos.y3;
                cacheTightenPos.Add(new Cog3DVect2(tempPosx, tempPosy));
            }
            else
            {
                tempPosx = -999.999;
                tempPosy = -999.999;
                cacheTightenPos.Add(new Cog3DVect2(tempPosx, tempPosy));
                tempPosx = -999.999;
                tempPosy = -999.999;
                cacheTightenPos.Add(new Cog3DVect2(tempPosx, tempPosy));
                tempPosx = -999.999;
                tempPosy = -999.999;
                cacheTightenPos.Add(new Cog3DVect2(tempPosx, tempPosy));
            }
            //SysPara.screwIndex == 6
            if (cacheTightenPos.Count >= 18)
            {

                if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.UpConveyorAutoFlow.BFirstOrSecond) //(SysPara.bFirstOrSecond)
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 0;
                else
                    SysPara.screwIndex = 18;
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else
            {
                return FCResultType.CASE1;
            }

        }

        private void btnPathSelect_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {

            FolderBrowserDialog fbDialog = new FolderBrowserDialog();
            if (fbDialog.ShowDialog() == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                // txtSavePath.Text = fbDialog.SelectedPath;
                DataTable dt = SettingData.Tables["Pset"];
                dt.Rows[0]["SaveImagePath"] = fbDialog.SelectedPath;
                dt.AcceptChanges();
            }
        }

        private FCResultType flowChart11_FlowRun_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                 MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_RightUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                 MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderRise.Off() &&
                 MiddleLayer.ConveyorF.IB_LeftUnderPreCylinderDrop.On() &&
                 IB_ScrewLockingCylinderRise.On() &&
                 IB_ScrewLockingCylinderDescend.Off())
            {
                SysPara.screwIndex += 1;
                string[] pos;
                string ScanPosName = "PosName" + SysPara.screwIndex;
                pos = GetDataByAnnotation(dgv_GrabPosition, ScanPosName);
                posX = double.Parse(pos[2]);
                posY = double.Parse(pos[3]);
                posZ = double.Parse(pos[4]);

                motorMoveCtrl.AlwaysUpToSafety = false;
                motorMoveCtrl.ZSafetyPos = posZ;
                motorMoveCtrl.StartMoveFlow(posX, posY, posZ);
                string str = posX.ToString() + "_" + posY.ToString() + "_" + posZ.ToString();
                CCDPosX = posX;
                CCDPosY = posY;
                //MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Production, str + "The axis moves to the screw-taking position:GantryFlow", true);
                return FCResultType.NEXT;
            }
            else { return FCResultType.IDLE; }
        }
