        //            ScrewData.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        //    });
        //    //});

        //}
        public void ShowImage( List<double> Torques, List<double> Angles, List<double> Times,string chart1_ScrewName, string chart1_SerialNumber,double St, double Pta, string pngFileName)
        {
            try
            {
                // Length Protection
                int count = Math.Min(Torques.Count, Math.Min(Angles.Count, Times.Count));
                if (count <= 1)
                {
                    return;
                }
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(ScrewData,() =>
                {
                        try
                        {
                            ScrewData.Titles[0].Text = $"Torque And Angle Curves\n" + $"{chart1_ScrewName} OF {chart1_SerialNumber}" + $" [Torques({St:f3}) Angles({Pta:f3})]";
                            ScrewData.SuspendLayout();
                            ScrewData.Series["Torque"] .Points.Clear();
                            ScrewData.Series["Angle"].Points.Clear();
                            for (int i = 1; i < count; i++)
                            {
                                ScrewData.Series["Torque"].Points.AddXY(Times[i],Torques[i]);
                                ScrewData.Series["Angle"].Points.AddXY( Times[i], Angles[i]);
                            }
                            ScrewData.ResumeLayout();
                            //Task.Run(() =>
                            //{
                            //    try
                            //    {
                            //        lock (ScrewData)
                            //        {
                                        ScrewData.SaveImage(pngFileName,ChartImageFormat.Png);
                            //        }
                            //    }
                            //    catch (Exception ex)
                            //    {
                            //        MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas,$"Chart Save Error:{ex}", true);
                            //    }
                            //});
                        }
                        catch (Exception ex)
                        {
                            MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas,$"Chart UI Error:{ex}",true);
                        }
                    });
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                MiddleLayer.LogF.AddLog(LogType.Alarmas,$"ShowImage Error:{ex}",true);
            }
        }


        #region FFU
        FFUCom ffu = new FFUCom();
        public bool ConnectFFU(string com, string para)
        {
            if (ffu.Connect(com, para))
            {
                return true;
            }
            else { return false; }
        }
        public bool OpenFFUMaxSpeep()
        {
            return ffu.OpenFFUMaxSpeed();
        }
        public void CloseFFU()
        {
            if (ffu.SetFFUSpeed(0))
            {
                //Thread.Sleep(500);
                DelayMs(500);
                ffu.DisConnect();
            }
        }
        //Connect FFU
        private void button11_Click_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            string comStr = MiddleLayer.GantryF.GetSettingValue("MSet", "FFUComPort");
            string paraStr = txb_FFURate.Text + "," + txb_FFUDataBit.Text + "," +
                txb_FFUEvenOrOrder.Text + "," + txb_FFUStopBIt.Text;
            if (ConnectFFU(comStr, paraStr))
            {
                this.button11.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else { this.button11.BackColor = Color.Red; }
        }
        //Disconnect FFU
        private void button10_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            ffu.DisConnect();
            this.button11.BackColor = Color.White;
        }
        //Set Max Speed
        private void button20_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ffu.OpenFFUMaxSpeed())
            {
                button20.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else { button20.BackColor = Color.Red; }
        }
        //Close FFU
        private void button9_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ffu.SetFFUSpeed(0))
            {
                button23.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else { button23.BackColor = Color.Red; }
        }
        //Set Speed
        private void button4_Click_1(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ffu.SetFFUSpeed((int)nud_FFUSpeed.Value))
            {
                button23.BackColor = Color.Green;
            }
            else { button23.BackColor = Color.Red; }
        }

        private void tabPage19_Enter(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            tm_ReadFFUSpeed.Start();
        }

        private void tabPage19_Leave(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            tm_ReadFFUSpeed.Stop();
        }

        private void tm_ReadFFUSpeed_Tick(object sender, EventArgs e)
        {
            if (ffu.ConnectStatus)
            {
                RefreshDifferentThreadUI(MiddleLayer.GantryF.txb_CurFFUSpeed, () =>
                {
                    MiddleLayer.GantryF.txb_CurFFUSpeed.Text = ffu.GetFFUSpeed().ToString();
                    //Thread.Sleep(100);
                    DelayMs(100);
                });
            }








        }
        #endregion
    }
}
